K18-7  Under the antecedents of Theorem 18-7(1) (A\_j defined by the actual filtered write, the common affine post-processing absorbed into B\_j), a difference that appears although the criterion is zero; or, on a linear time-invariant closed loop, a difference that appears later although the Cayley–Hamilton test is passed.  
K18-8  Under the setup of Theorem 18-8, an example in which the first component difference comes earlier than the beat fixed in (2)(A); or one in which, at a beat where the common conditions of (2)(B) hold, the actual post-state difference appears later than that beat or never appears; or one in which the overwrite antecedent of (4) (identical complete mathematical states after overwriting) holds and the two runs are separable afterwards; or one in which all four items of (5) are in place and the readings do not separate.  
K18-9  A case in which, under derived incidence, the read-out law and the write law are no longer functions of d while Theorems 18-1 to 18-3 are still invoked (to be judged a distortion); or, under the solvability antecedents of Theorem 18-9(2), a linear-conductance network with ΔQ\_x ≠ Q\_e·T⁰\_x(u→v).  
K18-10  (Real-object layer) For the breach conditions of PN-1–PN-5, PC-1, PC-2, PP-1, PP-3 and PP-4, see 9.2 and 9.3; a breach at the real-object layer breaches only the corresponding conditional prediction and its group of antecedents, not the model theorems.

13\. Condition Items and Status

### 13.1  Register of Antecedents

　U1  Activity uniqueness (D18-7): antecedent of Theorems 18-1(1), 18-8 and 18-10.  
　LW  Local write (D18-9): antecedent of Theorems 18-3, 18-5 and 18-7(1); it constrains only the write before booking.  
　LP  Position-wise post-processing (D18-1): antecedent of the clauses of Theorem 18-3 on the actual post-state.  
　PC\_g, PC\_g^F  Process pairing (D18-5): under booked-as-effective semantics (W^ε\_num) the antecedent of Theorems 18-2(1) and 18-9(3) is PC\_g; when the run uses the F-layer certified filtered write (W^ε\_F), it is PC\_g^F.  
　Quasi-static remainder and fixed structural branch: antecedents of Theorem 18-5(1)–(4); when they fail, the set-level linearization of (5) is used, limited to differentiability and a feasible neighborhood.  
　Valid process-by-process decomposition: antecedent of Theorem 18-4; without a decomposition, nothing is issued, per (3).  
　Frozen declared contrast family (including joint validity): antecedent of the preamble and of (2) of Theorem 18-4.  
　D12 law class of \[S2\]: antecedent of Theorem 18-4(2)(A) and (4)(A).  
　The three J₁₈ conditions (raw events and certifications, exact interpretation of the numerical values of both arms, the closed-graph condition declared by the evidence protocol, the D20 check of \[S2\] on the raw input, registration of formation and out-of-boundary causes by the evaluator of Appendix A.1 of \[S2\], nonnegative encoding of signed depth): antecedent of Theorem 18-4(4); the common domain (same pre-state, identical pulses and formation records, the F-layer domain, post not triggered): antecedent of the simplified form in (4)(B).  
　Restricted class (no residue, an actual filtered write of common affine form that defines A\_j, the common affine post-processing of both arms absorbed into B\_j, linear drive): antecedent of Theorem 18-7(1); linear time-invariant closed loop: antecedent of (4).  
　Law-layer setup (D18-11, c taken at the pre-beat state, Φ not reading c, the initial r of both arms identical and the residue recursion not reading c): antecedent of Theorem 18-8; (2)(B) carries in addition that post is the identity on the target component at the actual booked values of both arms (otherwise the difference is taken after the actual post-processing of both arms) and that booking does not cancel; setting and weak writes take effect according to the actual recursion ((4), (5)).  
　Linear-conductance network and solvability (fixed injection or the same boundary): antecedent of Theorem 18-9(2).

### 13.2  Status

　\[Derivation\] Theorems 18-1 to 18-5, 18-7 and 18-10 hold universally under their antecedents (18-2(1) has separate clauses for the original mechanism and for booking as effective, 18-3 separates two layers, before booking and the actual post-state, and 18-10 has as antecedent that the declared operators are evaluable); Theorem 18-6 is a correspondence and a structural analogy; Theorems 18-8 and 18-9 are tagged \[Conditional conclusion\].  
　\[Representational choice\] D18-1, D18-6 (tendency as the inclination layer of organization).  
　\[Conditional conclusion\] The derived items for the two representatives in 9.2 and 9.3; a read-back reading of 1 additionally requires the read-back antecedents of 8.2.  
　\[Prediction\] PN-1–PN-5, PC-1, PC-2, PP-1, PP-3, PP-4.  
　Programs: T1–T25 of Appendix B.2 are finite verification, construction witnesses and counterexamples; universal conclusions rest on the proofs listed in this section.

### 13.3  Three-Tier Register

　Theoretical conditions: the theorems under the antecedents of 13.1, and the derived items of 9.2 and 9.3.  
　Testable predictions: the predictions listed in 9.2 and 9.3.  
　Undetermined in reality: direct measurement at the α3 synaptic layer; whether consolidation is borne by history control or by clock control; whether the slime-mold adaptation rate changes with history; the C1–C6 budgets for joining records on the two representatives (\[17\] 8.2, 8.3).

15\. Interfaces  
Input: the identification output of \[17\] (T, depth bearing, the organization hypergraph and its boundaries, history-condition witnesses and cross-record-chain conclusions), used within the limits it states.  
Scope of inheritance: Chapters 19 to 21 and the Master Map inherit only the clause forms listed in this paper together with their antecedents — Theorem 18-2(1) with separate clauses for the original mechanism and for booking as effective (W^ε\_num requires PC\_g, W^ε\_F requires PC\_g^F); Theorem 18-3 with before booking and the actual post-state kept apart (the latter requires LP), and non-invasiveness defined by the actual post-state; the frozen contrast family and contrast-by-contrast certificates of the preamble of Theorem 18-4 (contrasts normalized to their actual difference sets, with unique identities; routes bound to the bearing positions in the actual difference set of that contrast; the aggregate Path used only as an independent atom), and the three J₁₈ conditions of (4) (exact interpretation of the values of both arms, the closed-graph condition declared by the evidence protocol, D20 checked first on the raw certifications, D19 evaluated by the evaluator of Appendix A.1 of \[S2\]); Theorem 18-7(1) with the restricted class in which A\_j is defined by the actual filtered write and B\_j is included; for Theorem 18-8, the antecedent that the initial complete states of the two arms relevant to read-out (including residue) are identical apart from c, the first target-depth relaxation component difference and the isolation conditions, the actual post-state difference with post-processing the identity or taken at actual values, and the antecedent that setting and weak writes take effect according to the actual recursion; Theorem 18-9(3), which requires PC\_g under booking as effective; Theorem 18-10, with the evaluability of the declared operators as antecedent.  
Output — an executable model of the seven-step research procedure: Sections 3 to 5 of this paper (definition sequence, equations, solver) and the programs of Appendix A constitute the executable model for Steps 4 to 6 of the concretized-field research procedure; 8.3 is a link-by-link account of the seven steps; 9.2 and 9.3 are complete runs on two representatives.  
　To Chapter 19, *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Compartmentalized Third-Factor Circuits* (biological neural hosts): the seven steps and the predictions of 9.2; the law-layer Trace-Response (Theorem 18-8) as the representation for research on consolidation; the loop-direction law (Theorem 18-5(3)) as a unified reading of return protection and self-limitation.  
　To Chapter 20, *The Discrimination Form of Trace-Response Transformation in Artificial Neural Networks* (artificial-neural-network hosts): the complete state of program hosts (weights as d, membrane potentials and eligibility traces as r); the three conditions of Theorem 18-4(4), which give the conditions under which program hosts and the F layer of \[S2\] can be used interchangeably (event-table fields, trajectory reproduction, witnesses and qualification evaluated separately); relabeling comparisons under booking as effective must check process pairing (Theorem 18-2(1)); the interface between read-back atoms and complete certificates (preamble of Theorem 18-4); Field Induction engineering can be realized as the design of ψ\_x and of loop gain.  
　To Chapter 21, *The Mathematical Form of Field-Law Generation in Geomorphic Concretized Fields* (riverbed deposition and flow-splitting hosts): the LW form of Exner-type writes; channel splitting and flow division as a cross-position comparison of ψ\_x; the scour and deposition regimes as regime dependence of the loop direction (Theorem 18-5(3)), and abandoned channels as H2 relative to the current flow; avulsion as a change of derived incidence, with the removal response computed under the actual boundary conditions (fixed potential or fixed injection) (Theorem 18-9); if riverbed post-processing (such as gridding or truncation) crosses positions, the locality conclusions issue only before booking (Theorem 18-3).  
　To later research directions (light–gravity hosts, chapter number not fixed): first look for Trace-Responses by the first link; if no change is found that is retained at the declared resolution and takes part in later action, the study stops at the first link.  
　To the final chapter, *The Master Map of the Field Concretization Group Form*: the concretized-field research procedure must be included in the Master Map; 8.3 of this paper is its link-by-link account on the formal side, and Sections 3 to 5 and Appendix A are the executable model for Steps 4 to 6.  
　To \[17\]: Theorem 18-4(2) gives, on the law class of \[S2\], a proof of what its Note 6′-1 states, that “the effective-difference certificate and functional support stand side by side and are not equivalent”, together with a separating example on an effective write; the preamble of Theorem 18-4 gives an item-by-item verification of the four clauses of Definition 6′ on the model interface.

## Appendix A  Program Source

Runtime environment: Python 3.10 or later, using only the standard library and exact rational arithmetic from fractions. The following 4 files are placed in the same directory: A.1 and A.2 are the libraries of this paper, A.3 is the run entry, and A.4 is the F-layer evaluator of \[S2\]. A.4 is verbatim identical to Appendix A.1 of \[S2\], *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* v1.0 (main-text SHA-256 98a76c56e4fc20b8e39e9843f32e5ffca77e135886f9a809e7625f5510ff4854), with file SHA-256 dfd11fe6866a508542363f9d32bd3bcf1e2f2917445ad61b8fcda78ab859acd1; the other programs of Appendix A call only its check (D20) and run (D19). The programs read no file outside Appendix A. The programs give finite verification, construction witnesses and counterexamples; universal conclusions rest on the proofs in the main text.  
A.1  jxs\_core.py  Core library: complete states and one-beat runs, the two resolution semantics, process pairing, tendency, exact dynamical identity, the permanent-hiding criterion for linear time-invariant closed loops, the F-layer law class of \[S2\] and the two readings of the history condition, the three-valued conjunction of D18, read-back atoms and complete certificates on frozen contrast families, the event table and three conditions of J₁₈, nonnegative encoding

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0: core library of the run system.
Corresponds to Sections 3–5 of the main text: the complete state σ = (d, r); the read-out law Φ; write components; the residue law P; the two resolution semantics;
tendency, affected set, action set; exact dynamical identity; the permanent-hiding criterion for linear time-invariant closed loops; F-layer compilation and the two readings of the history condition.
Discipline: standard library only; every numerical value is a fractions.Fraction; no random numbers.
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product

class Host:
    """Declared host model 𝔐 (D18-1).
    positions: list of Structural Positions with identity labels (X̂; may include latent positions controlled by existence variables).
    read(d, r, s) -> dict: full activity y (D18-7).
    comps(d, y, s) -> dict pos -> list[(process identifier, component c)]: valid process-by-process decomposition of the write law (D18-1).
    post(d_new, d_old) -> dict: declared post-processing (e.g. clipping, generation of existence positions); the identity if none is declared.
    resid(r, y) -> r': residue law; with no residue, r is always None.
    """
    def __init__(self, positions, read, comps, post=None, resid=None):
        self.positions = list(positions)
        self.read = read
        self.comps = comps
        self.post = post or (lambda dn, do: dn)
        self.resid = resid or (lambda r, y: r)

def step(host, d, r, s, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """One beat of the run (run equation). sem = 'raw': original-mechanism semantics, all components booked; 'booked': booked-as-effective semantics, only |c| > eps booked.
    Returns (y, d', r', record); the record contains the components at each position, Act⁰, Act^ε, Aff⁰ and Aff^ε."""
    y = host.read(d, r, s)
    cs = host.comps(d, y, s)
    dn = dict(d)
    for x, lst in cs.items():
        if sem == "raw":
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst)
        else:
            dn[x] = d[x] + sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)
    dn = host.post(dn, d)
    rn = host.resid(r, y)
    act0 = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(c != 0 for _, c in lst)}
    acte = {x for x, lst in cs.items() if any(abs(c) > eps for _, c in lst)}
    aff0 = {x for x in host.positions if dn.get(x, F(0)) != d.get(x, F(0))}
    affe = {x for x in host.positions if abs(dn.get(x, F(0)) - d.get(x, F(0))) > eps}
    return y, dn, rn, {"comps": cs, "Act0": act0, "Acte": acte, "Aff0": aff0, "Affe": affe}

def run(host, d, r, hist, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, d, r, _ = step(host, d, r, s, eps, sem)
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d, r

def potential(host, d, r, signals):
    """Tendency Φ_σ (D18-6): the full-activity response over the labeled signal class."""
    return {s: host.read(d, r, s) for s in signals}

def exact_dyn_same(host, s1, s2, alphabet, L, eps=F(0), sem="raw"):
    """Finite verification of exact dynamical identity (D18-14) over all histories of length ≤ L; returns (whether identical, number of histories).
    eps and sem apply to both the runs and the comparison: the default is original-mechanism semantics (sem = "raw"); booked-as-effective semantics must be passed explicitly as sem = "booked" together with ε."""
    n = 0
    for k in range(L + 1):
        for h in product(alphabet, repeat=k):
            n += 1
            y1, _, _ = run(host, s1[0], s1[1], h, eps, sem)
            y2, _, _ = run(host, s2[0], s2[1], h, eps, sem)
            if y1 != y2:
                return False, n
    return True, n

def booked_sum(lst, eps):
    """Component sum of the purely numerical filtered write W^ε_num: counts only components with |c| > ε (this is the operator of sem = "booked" in step)."""
    return sum(c for _, c in lst if abs(c) > eps)

def booked_sum_F(lst, eps, cert):
    """Component sum of the F-layer certified filtered write W^ε_F: counts only components with |c| > ε and cert_eff = 1 ([S2] 4.2). cert: {process: 1/0/'⊥'}, default 1."""
    return sum(c for rho, c in lst if abs(c) > eps and cert.get(rho, 1) == 1)

def pairing_ok(cs_a, cs_b, pi):
    """Sufficient condition for process-identity pairing (Theorem 18-2(1), booked-as-effective clause): for every position x, the multiset of (process, component) of state a at x
    equals that of state b at π(x) (values preserved component by component; the same threshold ε on both sides). This guarantees that the purely numerical filtered write W^ε_num commutes;
    the F-layer certified filtered write W^ε_F additionally requires equal certification states on both sides of the pairing (cert_pairing_ok)."""
    keys = set(cs_a) | {k for k in pi}
    for x in keys:
        la = sorted(cs_a.get(x, []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        lb = sorted(cs_b.get(pi.get(x, x), []), key=lambda t: (str(t[0]), t[1]))
        if la != lb:
            return False
    return True

def cert_pairing_ok(cs_a, cert_a, cert_b, pi):
    """The action-certification states on both sides of the pairing are equal: cert_eff of process ρ at position x equals cert_eff of the same-named process at position π(x). cert: {(process, position): value}."""
    for x, lst in cs_a.items():
        for rho, _ in lst:
            if cert_a.get((rho, x), 1) != cert_b.get((rho, pi.get(x, x)), 1):
                return False
    return True

def mat_mul(A, B):
    return [[sum(A[i][k] * B[k][j] for k in range(len(B))) for j in range(len(B[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]

def hidden_forever_lti(A, C, delta):
    """Linear time-invariant closed loop Δ' = AΔ with reading CΔ: by Cayley–Hamilton, checking k = 0..n−1 is the universal criterion (Theorem 18-7(4))."""
    v = [[x] for x in delta]
    for _ in range(len(A)):
        if any(row[0] != 0 for row in mat_mul(C, v)):
            return False
        v = mat_mul(A, v)
    return True

# ---- F-layer compilation and the two readings of the history condition (Theorem 18-4) ----
def f_component(law, s):
    """The three classes of numerical law of [S2] D12: ('const', b) / ('lin', b, k, R) / ('bil', b, k, j1, j2)."""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return law[1]
    if law[0] == "lin":
        _, b, k, R = law
        return b + k * sum(s[j] for j in R)
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    return b + k * s[j1] * s[j2]

def f_supp_act(law, s):
    """Functional support of [S2] D15 (mathematically nonzero, independent of ε)."""
    if law[0] == "const":
        return set()
    if law[0] == "lin":
        return set(law[3]) if law[2] != 0 else set()
    _, b, k, j1, j2 = law
    if k == 0:
        return set()
    out = set()
    if s[j2] != 0:
        out.add(j1)
    if s[j1] != 0:
        out.add(j2)
    return out

def histcond_F(law, s, T, eps, formed):
    """HistCond_F of [S2] D17, D18 (path certification taken as 1, cert_read taken as 1): available positions = the already formed positions in Supp_act ∩ T with s > ε."""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    return 1 if avail else 0

def histcond_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Numeric-difference reading with single-set contrasts: returns 1 if some valid contrast that changes only one formed position in T with depth > ε makes the total component difference > ε, and 0 otherwise.
    This tests the numerical part of [17] Definition 6′(C) on the single-set subprotocol; it is not a complete certificate. contrasts: table of legitimate values {position: [value…]}."""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for i in pos:
        for v in contrasts.get(i, []):
            s2 = dict(s); s2[i] = v
            if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                return 1
    return 0

# ---- HistCond_F with path and read certification; joint contrasts; two columns of raw judgments; F-layer booking (with cert_eff) ----
def histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """[S2] D18: HistCond_F = [Avail_act ≠ ∅] ∧ cert_read ∧ Path({(i,t) : i ∈ Avail_act}, m).
    path_from(start set) -> 1/0/'⊥': the path status, on the declared evidence-path graph, from that start set to this mechanism's event (D14)."""
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    if not avail:
        return 0
    if cert_read == 0:
        return 0
    p = path_from(frozenset(avail))
    if p == 0:
        return 0
    if cert_read == "⊥" or p == "⊥":
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Cartesian-closure enumeration (used when the declared family of jointly valid contrasts is explicitly the Cartesian closure of per-position candidate values): legitimate values are taken simultaneously on any nonempty subset of the formed positions in T with depth > ε.
    Returns 'numeric difference crosses threshold' or 'numeric difference does not cross threshold' (only the numerical part of [17] Definition 6′(C); the latter is not a negation either)."""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                if abs(f_component(law, s2) - base) > eps:
                    return "numeric difference crosses threshold"
    return "numeric difference does not cross threshold"

def readback_hist(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1):
    """Two columns of raw judgments: (value of the F-layer functional support, numeric-difference judgment under joint contrasts)."""
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    hd = histcond_diff_joint(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts)
    return ("functional support established" if hf == 1 else ("functional support undetermined" if hf == "⊥" else "functional support not established")), hd

def f_layer_update(comps, eps):
    """F-layer booking of [S2] 4.2: counts only the components with Act_F = [|c| > ε] ∧ cert_eff = 1. comps: [(c, cert_eff)]."""
    return sum(c for c, ce in comps if abs(c) > eps and ce == 1)

def raw_update(comps):
    return sum(c for c, _ in comps)

# ---- D18 three-valued conjunction; read-back atoms and three-column summary; F-layer contrast under the actual equation ----
def kleene_and(vals):
    """Three-valued conjunction: 0 if any item is 0; ⊥ if no item is 0 but some item is ⊥; 1 if all items are 1."""
    if any(v == 0 for v in vals):
        return 0
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 1

def max_contrast_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Maximum difference over the Cartesian closure: use only when the declared family of jointly valid contrasts is exactly the Cartesian closure of per-position candidate values (declared explicitly, never a default)."""
    from itertools import combinations, product as prod
    base = f_component(law, s)
    pos = [i for i in T if i in formed and s[i] > eps]
    best = F(0)
    for k in range(1, len(pos) + 1):
        for sub in combinations(pos, k):
            for vals in prod(*[contrasts.get(i, []) for i in sub]):
                s2 = dict(s)
                for i, v in zip(sub, vals):
                    s2[i] = v
                best = max(best, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    return best

def kleene_or(vals):
    """Three-valued disjunction: 1 if any item is 1; ⊥ if no item is 1 but some item is ⊥; 0 if all items are 0 (or there are none)."""
    if any(v == 1 for v in vals):
        return 1
    if any(v == "⊥" for v in vals):
        return "⊥"
    return 0

def contrast_family(contrasts):
    """Normalized contrast family (preamble of Theorem 18-4).
    contrasts as {position: [value…]}: single-set shorthand; each value at each position forms one single-set contrast, and no joint contrast is generated automatically; the declared coverage is recorded as “complete”.
    contrasts as {"contrast": [...], "coverage": "complete"/"incomplete"}: a frozen set of contrasts, each of the form {"id": name, "set": {position: value}, "evidence": {...}};
      a joint contrast must be listed explicitly as one entry (or generated by a joint-validity predicate and then listed).
    Returns (list of contrasts, coverage)."""
    if "contrast" in contrasts:
        fam = [dict(id=k.get("id", "contrast %d" % n), set=dict(k["set"]), evidence=dict(k.get("evidence", {})))
               for n, k in enumerate(contrasts["contrast"])]
        ids = [k["id"] for k in fam]
        if len(set(ids)) != len(ids):
            raise ValueError("duplicate contrast identity")
        return fam, contrasts.get("coverage", "complete")
    fam = []
    for i in sorted(contrasts, key=str):
        for v in contrasts[i]:
            fam.append(dict(id="%s→%s" % (i, v), set={i: v}, evidence={}))
    return fam, "complete"

def numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover):
    """Numeric-difference item: counts only the contrasts that satisfy [17] Definition 6′(B) (changing only formed positions in T with depth > ε).
    Values: “numeric difference crosses threshold”, “numeric difference nonzero, below threshold”, “numeric difference zero”; if none crosses the threshold and the declared coverage is incomplete, records “contrast domain insufficiently covered”."""
    base = f_component(law, s)
    m = F(0)
    for k in fam:
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        if all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set):
            s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
            m = max(m, abs(f_component(law, s2) - base))
    if m > eps:
        return "numeric difference crosses threshold"
    if cover != "complete":
        return "contrast domain insufficiently covered"
    return "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold" if m > 0 else "numeric difference zero"

def numeric_diff(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts):
    """Numeric-difference item: the numerical part of [17] Definition 6′(C), not a complete certificate (the contrast family is normalized by contrast_family)."""
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    return numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)

def readback_atoms(law, s, T, eps, formed, contrasts, path_from, cert_read=1, cert_eff=1, evidence=None,
                   independent=None):
    """Read-back atoms and three-column summary of Theorem 18-4.
    Contrast family: normalized by contrast_family; for each contrast, the difference and the certification are computed on the same beat, the same process, the same target and that contrast's identity, and the disjunction is then taken over complete witnesses;
    certification is bound to the very contrast that yields the threshold-crossing difference and is never stitched together across contrasts.
    Atoms: numerical support Avail, read certification cert_read, path Path, HistCond_F (the original conjunction of [S2] D18), Act_F (with cert_eff),
    numeric difference (numeric_diff_family), the per-contrast details, and the clause-by-clause evidence for the four clauses of [17] Definition 6′ (same-beat, bearing position, route, action).
    evidence: common evidence of the same F event {key: 1/0/'⊥'}, declared as applying to every contrast; a contrast's own “evidence” takes precedence clause by clause. Clause defaults:
    same-beat is given by the construction of the contrast and recorded as 1; bearing position is judged per contrast by clause (B) (1 if only formed positions in T with depth > ε are changed, 0 otherwise); route is bound to the bearing positions that
    the contrast actually changes—1 if the path from these starting points to this event is 1, '⊥' otherwise (a path of 0 does not exclude another material route; a separately declared
    valid actual route must be supplied independently, with its own certificate); the aggregate Path is only an independent atom and does not replace the per-contrast route; action takes Act_F.
    The contrast details record, per contrast, (contrast identity, |Δ|, that contrast's witness value, that contrast's actual four clauses); “Def. 6′ four clauses” takes the four clauses of the witness contrast (of the first contrast if there is no witness contrast).
    “certificate status”: the identity of the witness contrast if there is one; otherwise the value of the disjunction of the listed contrast certificates, with “declared domain not yet covered” added when the declared coverage is incomplete.
    Merging the action clause: the F atom Act_F is the action evidence of the same event.
      Act_F = 0 (explicit refutation): an external claim cannot override it; if the external claim is 1, an “evidence conflict” is recorded and the blockade stands per the original refutation; the clause takes 0.
      Act_F = 1: if the external claim is 0, an “evidence conflict” is likewise recorded and the clause takes 0 (conservative blockade); if the external claim is 1 or absent, the clause takes 1.
      Act_F = ⊥ (unproven): an explicit external certification can supply it (its value is taken, and the source is recorded as “explicit certification”); if the external input is explicitly ⊥, the source is recorded as “explicitly unproven”;
      if nothing is given, the source is recorded as “unproven” and the clause is ⊥.
    independent: a witness from a different mechanism or an independent R-layer witness, which must be {event identity, mode, certification basis, action}; it is listed separately under the atom “independent witness”
      and is not merged into the four clauses, the complete certificate or the summary of this F event; if fields are missing, “independent witness incomplete” is recorded.
    complete certificate = the three-valued disjunction, over the contrasts, of [|Δ| > ε ∧ four clauses]. The summary follows the three-way split of values in [17] Definition 6′: if the complete certificate is 1, it records
    “effective difference certified”; otherwise, if the numeric difference is nonzero and below threshold (with complete declared coverage) or HistCond_F = 1, it records “functional support established”; in all other cases it records
    “effective difference not certified”. The summary does not override the atoms; conflicts, unproven items and refutations are shown in the atoms “evidence conflict” and “action source”, respectively."""
    evidence = dict(evidence or {})
    fam, cover = contrast_family(contrasts)
    avail = {i for i in f_supp_act(law, s) & set(T) if s[i] > eps and i in formed}
    path = path_from(frozenset(avail)) if avail else 0
    hf = histcond_F_full(law, s, T, eps, formed, path_from, cert_read)
    c = f_component(law, s)
    act = kleene_and([1 if abs(c) > eps else 0, cert_eff])
    nd = numeric_diff_family(law, s, T, eps, formed, fam, cover)
    conflicts = []
    ext = evidence.get("action")
    if act == 0:
        act_item, src = 0, "F-atom refutation"
        if ext == 1:
            conflicts.append("action: F atom Act_F = 0 (explicit refutation) conflicts with external claim 1; blockade stands per the original refutation")
    elif act == 1:
        if ext == 0:
            act_item, src = 0, "external refutation"
            conflicts.append("action: F atom Act_F = 1 conflicts with external claim 0; conservative blockade")
        else:
            act_item, src = 1, "F atom"
    else:
        if ext is None:
            act_item, src = "⊥", "unproven"
        elif ext == "⊥":
            act_item, src = "⊥", "explicitly unproven"
        else:
            act_item, src = ext, "explicit certification"
    detail, certs, witness, wfour, first_four = [], [], None, None, None
    for k in fam:
        ev = k["evidence"]
        # actual difference set: keep only the positions whose value really differs from the pre-beat value (setting a position back to its pre-beat value is not a change)
        act_set = {i: v for i, v in k["set"].items() if v != s[i]}
        legal = all(i in T and i in formed and s[i] > eps for i in act_set)
        s2 = dict(s); s2.update(act_set)
        dk = abs(f_component(law, s2) - c)
        # the route is bound to the bearing positions this contrast actually changes: the path from these starting points to this event; the aggregate Path does not replace it
        bearers = frozenset(i for i in act_set if i in avail)
        p_k = path_from(bearers) if bearers else 0
        route_k = ev.get("route", evidence.get("route", 1 if p_k == 1 else "⊥"))
        four_k = {"same-beat": ev.get("same-beat", evidence.get("same-beat", 1)),
                  "bearing position": ev.get("bearing position", evidence.get("bearing position", 1)) if legal else 0,
                  "route": route_k, "action": act_item}
        ck = kleene_and([1 if dk > eps else 0] + list(four_k.values()))
        detail.append((k["id"], dk, ck, dict(four_k)))
        if first_four is None:
            first_four = dict(four_k)
        certs.append(ck)
        if ck == 1 and witness is None:
            witness, wfour = k["id"], dict(four_k)
    cert = kleene_or(certs)
    four = wfour if witness is not None else (first_four if first_four is not None else
           {"same-beat": evidence.get("same-beat", 1), "bearing position": 0, "route": "⊥", "action": act_item})
    status = ("witness contrast “%s”" % witness if witness is not None else
              "disjunction of listed contrast certificates = %s" % cert + ("; declared domain not yet covered" if cover != "complete" else ""))
    atoms = {"Avail": 1 if avail else 0, "cert_read": cert_read, "Path": path, "HistCond_F": hf,
             "Act_F": act, "numeric difference": nd, "Def. 6′ four clauses": four, "action source": src, "evidence conflict": conflicts,
             "contrast details": detail, "witness contrast": witness, "complete certificate": cert, "coverage": cover, "certificate status": status}
    if independent is not None:
        need = ("event identity", "mode", "certification basis", "action")
        atoms["independent witness"] = dict(independent) if all(k in independent for k in need) else "independent witness incomplete"
    if cert == 1:
        col = "effective difference certified"
    elif hf == 1 or nd == "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold":
        col = "functional support established"
    else:
        col = "effective difference not certified"
    return atoms, col

def f_post_match(post, d, raw_sum, f_sum):
    """Comparison under the actual equation in Theorem 18-4(4)(A): one original-mechanism beat post(d + Σorig) against one F-layer beat d + ΣF."""
    return post(d + raw_sum) == d + f_sum

# ---- J₁₈: verification through the [S2] F-layer evaluator, trajectory reproduction and qualification (Theorem 18-4(4)) ----
def _tri(v):
    return {1: True, 0: False, "⊥": None, True: True, False: False, None: None}[v]

def _untri(v):
    return {True: 1, False: 0, None: "⊥"}[v]

def j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """Compiles the declared events of one run of this paper into the Spec of [S2] Appendix A.1 (raw events, certifications, times and sources are passed explicitly, without compression).
    events: each entry is
      {"kind": "pulse", "name": event identifier, "beat": t, "target": position, "amount": a, "out-of-boundary": True/False, "occurs": 1/0/'⊥'}, or
      {"kind": "process", "name": event identifier, "beat": t, "target": position, "carrier inside": True/False, "law": 'constant'/'linear'/'bilinear',
       "reads": (position…), "coefficient": k, "bias": b, "cert_eff": 1/0/'⊥', "cert_read": 1/0/'⊥'}.
    links: [(source node, target node, certification)]; a node is 'state:position:beat' or an event identifier.
    closed_graph: whether the evidence graph is closed, which must be declared by the evidence protocol (True/False); if it is undeclared (None), nothing is compiled."""
    if closed_graph not in (True, False):
        raise ValueError("closure of the evidence graph not declared")
    import field_final as FF
    pulses = tuple(FF.Pulse(e["name"], e["beat"], e["target"], e["amount"], e.get("out-of-boundary", True), _tri(e.get("occurs", 1)))
                   for e in events if e["kind"] == "pulse")
    procs = tuple(FF.Process(e["name"], e["beat"], e["target"], e["carrier inside"], e["law"], tuple(e.get("reads", ())),
                             e.get("coefficient", F(0)), e.get("bias", F(0)), _tri(e.get("cert_eff", 1)), _tri(e.get("cert_read", 1)))
                  for e in events if e["kind"] == "process")
    lk = tuple(FF.Link(a, b, _tri(c)) for a, b, c in links)
    return FF.Spec(tuple(sites), tuple(initial), tuple(window), pulses, procs, lk, _tri(identity), _tri(trace_history),
                   closed_graph, eps, tuple(T))

def j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events):
    """The same run under this paper's original-mechanism semantics (post is the identity): at each beat every process computes its component from the pre-beat state by its law, occurring pulses are added, and all components are booked.
    Every number entering the computation is first normalized by exact of [S2] (a decimal is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation), the same interpretation as in the upstream evaluator."""
    from field_final import exact
    st = {x: exact(v) for x, v in zip(sites, initial)}
    traj = []
    for t in range(window[0] - 1, window[-1] + 1):
        before = dict(st)
        add = {x: F(0) for x in sites}
        for e in events:
            if e["beat"] != t:
                continue
            if e["kind"] == "pulse":
                if e.get("occurs", 1) == 1:
                    add[e["target"]] += exact(e["amount"])
            else:
                rd = e.get("reads", ()); k = exact(e.get("coefficient", F(0))); b = exact(e.get("bias", F(0)))
                v = b if e["law"] == "constant" else (b + k * sum(before[q] for q in rd) if e["law"] == "linear"
                                                     else b + k * before[rd[0]] * before[rd[1]])
                add[e["target"]] += v
        st = {x: before[x] + add[x] for x in sites}
        if t in window:
            traj.append(dict(st))
    return traj

def j18_check(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links=(), identity=1, trace_history=1, closed_graph=None):
    """The three conditions of Theorem 18-4(4), evaluated through the [S2] evaluator (field_final.check, run):
    (A) an F candidate event table can be generated: j18_spec compiles (whether the evidence graph is closed is declared by the evidence protocol), and field_final.check accepts the raw certifications and raw events under D20;
         when D20 rejects (e.g. the action certification is undetermined, even for a zero component), F is not run and the R layer is reported separately as pending;
    (B) the numerical trajectory of the original run is reproduced: the post-states at each beat of the same original-mechanism run equal, position by position, the post-states at each beat reported by the F run;
    (C) witnesses and qualification are preserved: field_final.run computes Trace_F, Part_F, HDyn_F and Q_F of D19 and the per-event atoms."""
    import field_final as FF
    try:
        spec = j18_spec(sites, initial, window, eps, T, events, links, identity, trace_history, closed_graph)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject: %s" % exc, "R": "must be declared by the evidence protocol before evaluation", "event table": 0, "trajectory": "not run",
                "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    try:
        FF.check(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject: %s" % exc, "R": "R layer pending (evaluated separately by R-layer event witnesses)", "event table": 0, "trajectory": "not run",
                "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    try:
        out = FF.run(spec)
    except ValueError as exc:
        return {"D20": "reject (left the domain during the run): %s" % exc, "R": "R layer pending (evaluated separately by R-layer event witnesses)", "event table": 1,
                "trajectory": "not run", "Trace_F": "not run", "Part_F": "not run", "HDyn_F": "not run", "Q_F": "not run", "events": []}
    f_traj = [rep["after"] for rep in out["reports"]]
    raw = j18_raw_run(sites, initial, window, events)
    traj = 1 if [{x: F(v) for x, v in a.items()} for a in f_traj] == raw else 0
    evs = [dict(id=e["id"], c=e["delta"], Act_F=_untri(e["act"]), Cond_F=_untri(e["cond"]), HistCond_F=_untri(e["histcond"]))
           for rep in out["reports"] for e in rep["events"]]
    return {"D20": "accept", "R": "—", "event table": 1, "trajectory": traj, "Trace_F": _untri(out["trace"]), "Part_F": _untri(out["part"]),
            "HDyn_F": _untri(out["hdyn"]), "Q_F": _untri(out["Q"]), "events": evs, "F trajectory": f_traj, "original-mechanism trajectory": raw}

def nonneg_encode(d):
    """Nonnegative encoding of signed depth for entry into the F layer: d = d⁺ − d⁻ with d⁺, d⁻ ≥ 0; returns (encoding, read-back function)."""
    enc = {}
    for x, v in d.items():
        enc[(x, "+")] = v if v > 0 else F(0)
        enc[(x, "−")] = -v if v < 0 else F(0)
    return enc, (lambda e: {x: e[(x, "+")] - e[(x, "−")] for x in d})
~~~

A.2  jxs\_models.py  Declared host models: the minimal model of the *Drosophila* mushroom-body type, the *Drosophila* model with a consolidation state (history-controlled and clock-controlled), the Tero-type flow network, containment chains with existence variables, the γ1 self-loop toy, general-boundary conductance networks and incremental response

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0: declared host models (constructed in this paper; the parameters are constructed values, not measured values).
FLY: minimal model of the Drosophila mushroom-body type (KC × compartment synaptic depth; the MBON drives and the PPL1-α3 response make up the full activity).
FLYC: FLY plus the α3 consolidation state (law-layer Trace-Response, Theorem 18-8).
TERO: Tero-type flow network (conductance is the Trace-Response; flow rates and pressures are activity).
CHAIN: one-dimensional containment chain with existence variables (representation of Edge-Seepage, Theorem 18-9).
LOOP: γ1 self-loop toy (sign law of the local loop gain, Theorem 18-5).
"""
from fractions import Fraction as F
# ---------------- FLY ----------------
KC = [0, 1, 2, 3]
COMP = ("g1", "a3", "P", "N")
ODOR = {"A": (0, 1), "B": (2, 3), "C": (1, 2)}
A_INN, B_DIS, ETA, R1 = F(3, 4), F(1), F(1, 4), F(9, 10)
FLY_POS = [(i, c) for i in KC for c in COMP]

def fly_zero():
    return {p: F(0) for p in FLY_POS}

def fly_read(d, r, s):
    """Full activity: the MBON drive of each compartment and the PPL1-α3 response; rest R has no activity reading (returns empty)."""
    if s == "R":
        return {}
    y = {"MBON_" + c: sum(1 - d[(i, c)] for i in ODOR[s]) for c in COMP}
    y["PPL1_a3"] = -A_INN + B_DIS * sum(d[(i, "g1")] for i in ODOR[s])
    return y

def fly_comps(d, y, s):
    """Write components: when s is presented, the α3 column (K(s)) receives η·PPL1-α3; every beat, γ1 relaxes by −(1 − r1)·d."""
    cs = {}
    if s != "R":
        for i in ODOR[s]:
            cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("W_a3", ETA * y["PPL1_a3"]))
    for i in KC:
        cs.setdefault((i, "g1"), []).append(("RELAX_g1", -(1 - R1) * d[(i, "g1")]))
    return cs

def fly_post(dn, do):
    """Clipping: the α3 positions are clipped to [0, 1]."""
    out = dict(dn)
    for i in KC:
        out[(i, "a3")] = min(F(1), max(F(0), out[(i, "a3")]))
    return out

def make_fly():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(FLY_POS, fly_read, fly_comps, fly_post)

# ---------------- FLYC: consolidation state ----------------
# Each α3 position also has a consolidation state c ∈ {0, 1} (law-layer Trace-Response); the per-beat retention ratio of α3 depression is ρ(c): c = 0 → 1/2, c = 1 → 9/10.
# Consolidation write (history version): when s is presented and PPL1-α3 > θ_c, c ← 1 for the α3 positions of K(s). Clock-controlled version: c ← 1 once k_c beats have elapsed since the first write.
RHO = {0: F(1, 2), 1: F(9, 10)}
THETA_C = F(1, 8)

def flyc_positions():
    return FLY_POS + [("c", i) for i in KC]

def flyc_zero():
    z = fly_zero()
    for i in KC:
        z[("c", i)] = F(0)
    return z

def flyc_read(d, r, s):
    return fly_read({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, r, s)

def flyc_comps_history(d, y, s):
    cs = fly_comps({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, y, s)
    for i in KC:
        rho = RHO[int(d[("c", i)])]
        cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("RELAX_a3", -(1 - rho) * d[(i, "a3")]))
    if s != "R" and y["PPL1_a3"] > THETA_C:
        for i in ODOR[s]:
            if d[("c", i)] == 0:
                cs.setdefault(("c", i), []).append(("CONSOL", F(1)))
    return cs

def make_flyc(clock=None, block_dan=False):
    """clock=None: history version; clock=k: clock-controlled version (r counts the beats since the first α3 write). block_dan: DAN blockade during consolidation (neither the PPL1-α3 write nor the consolidation write occurs)."""
    from jxs_core import Host
    def comps(d, y, s):
        if block_dan and s != "R":
            y = dict(y); y["PPL1_a3"] = F(0)
        cs = flyc_comps_history(d, y, s) if clock is None else None
        if clock is not None:
            cs = fly_comps({p: d[p] for p in FLY_POS}, y, s)
            for i in KC:
                rho = RHO[int(d[("c", i)])]
                cs.setdefault((i, "a3"), []).append(("RELAX_a3", -(1 - rho) * d[(i, "a3")]))
        if block_dan and s != "R":
            for i in ODOR[s]:
                cs[(i, "a3")] = [p for p in cs.get((i, "a3"), []) if p[0] != "W_a3"]
        return cs
    def resid(r, y):
        if clock is None:
            return r
        return r  # in the clock-controlled version, timing is handled by post
    def post(dn, do):
        out = fly_post(dn, do)
        return out
    return Host(flyc_positions(), flyc_read, comps, post, resid)

def flyc_clock_run(d, hist, k_c, block_dan=False):
    """Explicit run of the clock-controlled consolidation version: c_i is set to 1 once k_c beats have elapsed since its α3 position first exceeded 0, regardless of whether DAN is active during consolidation."""
    from jxs_core import step
    host = make_flyc(clock=k_c, block_dan=block_dan)
    since = {i: None for i in KC}
    ys = []
    for s in hist:
        y, dn, _, _ = step(host, d, None, s)
        for i in KC:
            if since[i] is None and dn[(i, "a3")] > 0:
                since[i] = 0
            elif since[i] is not None:
                since[i] += 1
                if since[i] >= k_c:
                    dn[("c", i)] = F(1)
        d = dn
        ys.append(y)
    return ys, d

# ---------------- TERO ----------------
def solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    comp, stack = {snk}, [snk]
    while stack:
        x = stack.pop()
        for yv in adj[x]:
            if yv not in comp:
                comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
    if src not in comp:
        raise ValueError("source and sink are not connected")
    unk = [n for n in nodes if n in comp and n != snk]
    idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
    m = len(unk)
    A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
    b = [F(0)] * m
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
            continue
        for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
            if x in idx:
                A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                if yv in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
    b[idx[src]] = F(I)
    for col in range(m):
        piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
        A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
        for r in range(m):
            if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                for k in range(col, m):
                    A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                b[r] -= fct * b[col]
    p = {n: None for n in nodes}
    p[snk] = F(0)
    for n in unk:
        p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I):
    p = solve(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
    Q = []
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd <= 0 or p[u] is None or p[v] is None:
            Q.append(F(0))
        else:
            Q.append(dd * (p[u] - p[v]))
    return p, Q

def f_tero2(q):
    return q * q / (1 + q * q)

def make_tero(nodes, edges, src, snk, I, h, gam, eps_rm, grid=None, f=f_tero2):
    """Positions = tube-segment indices; d is the conductance (declared D⁰ = 0); activity = the flow rate in each tube and the node pressures; write h(f(|Q|) − γD);
    post: gridding and the removal threshold (conductance below eps_rm is set to 0: the shallowing limit, where the derived incidence changes, Theorem 18-9). The signal s is a multiple of the injection strength ('I' is constant injection)."""
    from jxs_core import Host
    pos = list(range(len(edges)))
    def read(d, r, s):
        D = [d[k] for k in pos]
        p, Q = flows(nodes, edges, D, src, snk, I)
        return {"Q": tuple(Q), "p": tuple(p[n] for n in nodes)}
    def comps(d, y, s):
        return {k: [("ADAPT", h * (f(abs(y["Q"][k])) - gam * d[k]))] for k in pos if d[k] > 0}
    def post(dn, do):
        out = dict(dn)
        for k in pos:
            v = out[k]
            if grid is not None:
                v = F(round(v * grid), grid)
            out[k] = v if v >= eps_rm else F(0)
        return out
    return Host(pos, read, comps, post)

# ---------------- CHAIN: representation of Edge-Seepage ----------------
# Latent positions X̂ = {0, 1, 2, 3}; existence variables e_x ∈ {0,1} and depths d_x ∈ [0, CAP]; the signal 'S' injects 1/2 into the first position of the existing chain that is not full;
# when position x is full (d_x = CAP) and the signal continues, x+1 is generated if it does not exist (e_{x+1} ← 1): a new Structural Position is generated along the edge when full (minimal representation of Equation 10 of The Mathematical Form of the Field).
CAP = F(1)

def chain_positions():
    return [("d", x) for x in range(4)] + [("e", x) for x in range(4)]

def chain_zero():
    z = {p: F(0) for p in chain_positions()}
    z[("e", 0)] = F(1)
    return z

def chain_read(d, r, s):
    present = [x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1]
    # Activity: the passage depth of signal S (which position it enters) and the outflow reading of each existing position (the deeper d_x, the easier the outflow: reading = d_x)
    if s == "R":
        return {"out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}
    tgt = next((x for x in present if d[("d", x)] < CAP), None)
    return {"tgt": tgt, "out": tuple(d[("d", x)] if x in present else None for x in range(4))}

def chain_comps(d, y, s):
    cs = {}
    if s == "S":
        tgt = y["tgt"]
        if tgt is not None:
            room = CAP - d[("d", tgt)]
            cs[("d", tgt)] = [("FILL", min(F(1, 2), room))]
        else:
            last = max(x for x in range(4) if d[("e", x)] == 1)
            if last + 1 < 4:
                cs[("e", last + 1)] = [("BIRTH", F(1))]
    return cs

def make_chain():
    from jxs_core import Host
    return Host(chain_positions(), chain_read, chain_comps)

# ---------------- LOOP: γ1 self-loop toy (sign law) ----------------
# A single γ1 subset depth s ∈ [0, 1]; MBON drive m = 2(1 − s), M fires ⇔ m ≥ θ_M; DAN drive = c + u·[M fires], D fires ⇔ ≥ θ_D;
# unreinforced CS+ beat: if D fires, write +A (depression deepens, clipped at 1); otherwise only shallowing by ×(1 − ρ).
def loop_step(s, u, c=F(1, 2), thM=F(1), thD=F(1, 2), A=F(1, 4), rho=F(1, 10)):
    m = 2 * (1 - s)
    gM = 1 if m >= thM else 0
    gD = 1 if c + u * gM >= thD else 0
    s2 = (1 - rho) * s + (A if gD else 0)
    return min(F(1), s2), gM, gD

# ---------------- Conductance network with general boundaries (Theorem 18-9(2)) ----------------
def components(nodes, edges, D):
    adj = {n: set() for n in nodes}
    for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
        if dd > 0:
            adj[u].add(v); adj[v].add(u)
    seen, out = set(), []
    for n in nodes:
        if n in seen:
            continue
        comp, stack = {n}, [n]
        while stack:
            x = stack.pop()
            for yv in adj[x]:
                if yv not in comp:
                    comp.add(yv); stack.append(yv)
        seen |= comp
        out.append(comp)
    return out

def solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    """Steady state of the conductance network under general boundaries: fixed = {node: fixed potential}, inj = {node: injection} (free nodes only).
    Solved component by component: a component containing a fixed-potential node is uniquely solvable; a component without one requires the injections within it to sum to 0 (conservation), otherwise ValueError;
    a component without a fixed-potential node sets the potential of its minimal node to 0 (the flow rates do not depend on this gauge)."""
    p = {}
    for comp in components(nodes, edges, D):
        fx = [n for n in comp if n in fixed]
        if not fx:
            if sum(F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in comp) != 0:
                raise ValueError("sources and sinks of the component are not conserved; the steady state has no solution")
            ref = min(comp, key=str)
            known = {ref: F(0)}
        else:
            known = {n: F(fixed[n]) for n in fx}
        unk = sorted([n for n in comp if n not in known], key=str)
        idx = {n: k for k, n in enumerate(unk)}
        m = len(unk)
        A = [[F(0)] * m for _ in range(m)]
        b = [F(inj.get(n, 0)) for n in unk]
        for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D):
            if dd <= 0 or u not in comp:
                continue
            for x, yv in ((u, v), (v, u)):
                if x in idx:
                    A[idx[x]][idx[x]] += dd
                    if yv in idx:
                        A[idx[x]][idx[yv]] -= dd
                    else:
                        b[idx[x]] += dd * known[yv]
        for col in range(m):
            piv = next(r for r in range(col, m) if A[r][col] != 0)
            A[col], A[piv] = A[piv], A[col]; b[col], b[piv] = b[piv], b[col]
            for r in range(m):
                if r != col and A[r][col] != 0:
                    fct = A[r][col] / A[col][col]
                    for k in range(col, m):
                        A[r][k] -= fct * A[col][k]
                    b[r] -= fct * b[col]
        p.update(known)
        for n in unk:
            p[n] = b[idx[n]] / A[idx[n]][idx[n]]
    return p

def flows_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj):
    p = solve_bc(nodes, edges, D, fixed, inj)
    return [dd * (p[u] - p[v]) if dd > 0 else F(0) for (u, v), dd in zip(edges, D)]

def removal_response(nodes, edges, D, fixed, k):
    """Incremental transfer under the same boundary: after the k-th edge e = (u, v) is removed, the increments at fixed-potential nodes stay 0 (homogeneous boundary);
    returns the flow rate on each edge when, among the free nodes, u injects a unit flow and v withdraws a unit flow (injection at a fixed-potential end is absorbed by the boundary and not counted)."""
    u, v = edges[k]
    Dc = list(D); Dc[k] = F(0)
    inj = {}
    if u not in fixed:
        inj[u] = F(1)
    if v not in fixed:
        inj[v] = inj.get(v, F(0)) - 1
    return flows_bc(nodes, edges, Dc, {n: F(0) for n in fixed}, inj)
~~~

A.3  jxs\_run.py  Run entry: sections T1–T25

~~~python
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Appendix program of The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0: run entry.
Run: python3 jxs_run.py; the exit code is 1 if any item does not match.
Status: finite verifications, construction witnesses and counterexamples; universal conclusions rest on the proofs of the theorems in the main text.
"""
import sys
from fractions import Fraction as F
from itertools import product, permutations
from pathlib import Path
HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import jxs_core as C  # noqa: E402
import jxs_models as M  # noqa: E402
RES = []

def check(tag, cond, note=""):
    RES.append(bool(cond))
    print(("  [pass] " if cond else "  [mismatch] ") + tag + (": " + note if note else ""))

def fs(x):
    x = F(x)
    return str(x.numerator) if x.denominator == 1 else "%d/%d" % (x.numerator, x.denominator)

def main():
    print("The Mathematical Form of Trace-Response Set Transformation v1.0: appendix program run output")
    print("=" * 60)
    fly = M.make_fly()
    # ---------------- T1 Five layers: layering of tendency, activity, Trace-Response and residue (Theorem 18-1) ----------------
    print("T1  Five layers: layering of tendency, activity, Trace-Response and residue (Theorem 18-1)")
    Xn = M.fly_zero(); Xn[(2, "g1")] = Xn[(3, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    Yn = M.fly_zero(); Yn[(0, "g1")] = Yn[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    yXn, dXn, _, _ = C.step(fly, Xn, None, "A")
    yYn, dYn, _, _ = C.step(fly, Yn, None, "A")
    check("T1a same depth multiset, different placement: under the same signal A the activities differ (MBON-γ1(A) = %s vs %s), and the successors of the α3 Trace-Response differ (d(0,a3) = %s vs %s)" %
          (fs(yXn["MBON_g1"]), fs(yYn["MBON_g1"]), fs(dXn[(0, "a3")]), fs(dYn[(0, "a3")])),
          sorted(Xn.values()) == sorted(Yn.values()) and yXn != yYn and dXn[(0, "a3")] != dYn[(0, "a3")])
    X0 = M.fly_zero(); X0[(0, "g1")] = F(3, 4); X0[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2); X0[(2, "a3")] = F(1, 4)
    Tn = M.fly_zero(); Tn[(0, "g1")] = F(1, 2); Tn[(2, "a3")] = F(1, 4)
    check("T1b same Trace-Response set, different signals: the activities differ (values of the tendency at different signals), and the read-out itself does not change the Trace-Response",
          M.fly_read(Tn, None, "A") != M.fly_read(Tn, None, "B") and Tn == dict(Tn))
    p1n, p1e = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4], [(0, 1), (1, 3), (0, 2), (2, 3), (1, 4)]
    DX, DY = [F(2), F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1)], [F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1), F(2)]
    _, QXn = M.flows(p1n, p1e, DX, 0, 3, 1)
    _, QYn = M.flows(p1n, p1e, DY, 0, 3, 1)
    check("T1c flow network (with the dangling branch tube (1,4)): same conductance multiset, different placement; under the same injection the flow distributions differ: %s vs %s; no flow in the dangling tube" % ([fs(q) for q in QXn], [fs(q) for q in QYn]),
          sorted(DX) == sorted(DY) and QXn != QYn and QYn[4] == 0)
    p4n, p4e = [0, 1, 2], [(0, 1), (0, 2)]
    DX4, DY4 = [F(1), F(1)], [F(1), F(3)]
    pXa, QXa = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DX4, 0, 1, 1)
    pYa, QYa = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DY4, 0, 1, 1)
    pXb, _ = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DX4, 2, 1, 1)
    pYb, _ = M.flows(p4n, p4e, DY4, 2, 1, 1)
    check("T1d same full activity under the actual signal, different tendency: under the actual injection (source 0) the two states have the same flows and pressures; with node 2 as the probe source the pressure differences are %s vs %s" %
          (fs(pXb[2]), fs(pYb[2])), QXa == QYa and pXa == pYa and pXb[2] != pYb[2])
    th1 = F(1)
    phi2 = lambda d, rr, sg: 1 if d + rr + sg >= th1 else 0
    P2 = lambda rr, y: F(1, 2) * rr + F(1, 4) * y
    sq0, sq1, r0, r1 = [], [], F(0), F(3, 4)
    for _ in range(4):
        y0, y1 = phi2(F(1, 2), r0, F(0)), phi2(F(1, 2), r1, F(0))
        sq0.append(y0); sq1.append(y1)
        r0, r1 = P2(r0, y0), P2(r1, y1)
    check("T1e residue enters the run semantics: same d = 1/2, same input 0; the activity sequences for r = 0 and 3/4 are %s vs %s, so the tendency must take (d, r) as its argument" % (sq0, sq1),
          sq0 != sq1 and phi2(F(1, 2), F(0), F(0)) != phi2(F(1, 2), F(3, 4), F(0)))
    inv_ok = True
    for i_d in range(8):
        for i_s in range(8):
            d_, sg, rr = F(i_d, 8), F(i_s, 8), F(0)
            if d_ + sg >= 1:
                continue
            for _ in range(6):
                rr = P2(rr, phi2(d_, rr, sg))
                if rr != 0:
                    inv_ok = False
    check("T1f invariant domain on which r can be uniquely eliminated {r = 0, d + s < 1, input constantly s}: on the grid r stays 0 for 6 beats; outside the domain, d = s = 1/2 gives r = %s after one beat" %
          fs(P2(F(0), phi2(F(1, 2), F(0), F(1, 2)))), inv_ok and P2(F(0), phi2(F(1, 2), F(0), F(1, 2))) != 0)
    G1n = M.fly_zero(); G1n[(0, "g1")] = G1n[(1, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    _, dG1, _, _ = C.step(fly, G1n, None, "A")
    _, dG0, _, _ = C.step(fly, G1n, None, "R")
    check("T1g probing is writing: with the write gate open, reading the tendency with A changes the α3 Trace-Response (d(0,a3): 0 → %s); rest neither reads nor writes" % fs(dG1[(0, "a3")]),
          dG1[(0, "a3")] != 0 and dG0[(0, "a3")] == 0)
    # ---------------- T2 Five layers and identity (Theorems 18-1, 18-2) ----------------
    print("T2  Relabeling equivariance and the two identities (Theorem 18-2; including a finite cross-check of the U, V induction)")
    X = M.fly_zero(); X[(0, "g1")] = X[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    Y = M.fly_zero(); Y[(2, "g1")] = Y[(3, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    check("T2a same depth multiset, different placement: the tendencies at A differ", C.potential(fly, X, None, "A") != C.potential(fly, Y, None, "A"))
    U = M.fly_zero(); U[(0, "g1")] = F(1)
    V = M.fly_zero(); V[(0, "g1")] = V[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    same, n = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (U, None), (V, None), "ABR", 6)
    check("T2b U and V are exactly dynamically identical on all %d ABR histories of length ≤ 6 (universal claim: the induction of Theorem 18-2)" % n, same and n == 1093)
    same_c, _ = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (U, None), (V, None), "ABCR", 1)
    check("T2c adding the overlapping odor C separates them (dynamical identity is relative to the input protocol)", not same_c)
    def g_apply(pi, tau, d):
        return {(pi[i], tau[c]): v for (i, c), v in d.items()}
    G4 = []
    for pk in permutations(M.KC):
        pi = dict(zip(M.KC, pk))
        for so in permutations("ABC"):
            sig = dict(zip("ABC", so)); sig["R"] = "R"
            if all(sorted(pi[i] for i in M.ODOR[s]) == sorted(M.ODOR[sig[s]]) for s in "ABC"):
                for tau in ({c: c for c in M.COMP}, {"g1": "g1", "a3": "a3", "P": "N", "N": "P"}):
                    G4.append((pi, sig, tau))
    ok = True
    for pi, sig, tau in G4:
        for L in range(4):
            for h in product("ABCR", repeat=L):
                y1, _, _ = C.run(fly, X0, None, h)
                y2, _, _ = C.run(fly, g_apply(pi, tau, X0), None, tuple(sig[s] for s in h))
                ren = [{("MBON_" + tau[k[5:]] if k.startswith("MBON_") else k): v for k, v in y.items()} for y in y1]
                if ren != y2:
                    ok = False
    check("T2d declared relabeling subgroup G4 (|G4| = %d): equivariant element by element on all histories of length ≤ 3" % len(G4), ok and len(G4) == 4)
    def in_g4(d1, d2):
        return any(g_apply(pi, tau, d1) == d2 for pi, sig, tau in G4)
    def acts(d, h):
        return C.run(fly, d, None, h)[0]
    def ren_same(d1, d2, L, alpha):
        for pi, sig, tau in G4:
            good = True
            for n_ in range(1, L + 1):
                for h in product(alpha, repeat=n_):
                    y1 = acts(d1, h)
                    y2 = acts(d2, tuple(sig[x] for x in h))
                    ren = [{("MBON_" + tau[k[5:]] if k.startswith("MBON_") else k): v for k, v in y.items()} for y in y1]
                    if ren != y2:
                        good = False; break
                if not good:
                    break
            if good:
                return True
        return False
    Xo = M.fly_zero(); Xo[(0, "g1")] = Xo[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    PIo = {0: 3, 1: 2, 2: 1, 3: 0}
    Yo = {(PIo[i], c): v for (i, c), v in Xo.items()}
    check("T2e cross-convention: X and its (0 3)(1 2) image Y are identical up to relabeling; at the fixed label A their tendencies differ (MBON-γ1(A) = %s vs %s), and after relabeling by σ they agree; exact dynamical identity fails (length ≤ 2), dynamical identity up to relabeling holds (length ≤ 3)" %
          (fs(M.fly_read(Xo, None, "A")["MBON_g1"]), fs(M.fly_read(Yo, None, "A")["MBON_g1"])),
          in_g4(Xo, Yo) and M.fly_read(Xo, None, "A") != M.fly_read(Yo, None, "A") and M.fly_read(Xo, None, "A") == M.fly_read(Yo, None, "B")
          and not C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (Xo, None), (Yo, None), "ABCR", 2)[0] and ren_same(Xo, Yo, 3, "ABCR"))
    check("T2f exact convention: U ≠ V yet they are exactly dynamically identical (T2b); the multisets of element values of U and V differ, so they lie on no common relabeling orbit", U != V and not in_g4(U, V))
    Gu = M.fly_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Gu[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    Ua = dict(Gu); Ua[(0, "a3")] = F(1)
    Va = dict(Gu); Va[(0, "a3")] = Va[(1, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    ya1, ua1, _, _ = C.step(fly, Ua, None, "A")
    yb1, va1, _, _ = C.step(fly, Va, None, "A")
    ya2 = M.fly_read(ua1, None, "A"); yb2 = M.fly_read(va1, None, "A")
    check("T2g Ua, Va (α3 depths 1, 0 vs 1/2 each): on the first presentation of A the full activities agree, on the second they differ because of clipping (MBON-α3(A) = %s vs %s): same current tendency, but not dynamically identical" %
          (fs(ya2["MBON_a3"]), fs(yb2["MBON_a3"])), ya1 == yb1 and ya2 != yb2)
    now_ren = any(all({("MBON_" + tau[k[5:]] if k.startswith("MBON_") else k): v for k, v in M.fly_read(Ua, None, x).items()}
                      == M.fly_read(Va, None, sig[x]) for x in "AB") for pi, sig, tau in G4)
    check("T2h relabeling convention (signal class {A, B, R}): Ua and Va have the same current tendency up to relabeling, yet every g in G4 separates them within length ≤ 2; when the signal class contains the overlapping odor C they are separable at once",
          now_ren and not ren_same(Ua, Va, 2, "ABR") and M.fly_read(Ua, None, "C") != M.fly_read(Va, None, "C"))
    TAUS = {"g1": "a3", "a3": "g1", "P": "P", "N": "N"}
    Xs = M.fly_zero(); Xs[(0, "g1")] = Xs[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    Xt = {(i, TAUS[c]): v for (i, c), v in Xs.items()}
    check("T2i the γ1↔α3 swap preserves the KC×compartment connectivity graph but is not in G4, and the two states have different full activities under A (PPL1-α3(A) = %s vs %s): isomorphism of connectivity graphs does not suffice for organizational identity" %
          (fs(M.fly_read(Xs, None, "A")["PPL1_a3"]), fs(M.fly_read(Xt, None, "A")["PPL1_a3"])),
          {(i, TAUS[c]) for (i, c) in M.FLY_POS} == set(M.FLY_POS) and all(t != TAUS for _, _, t in G4)
          and M.fly_read(Xs, None, "A") != M.fly_read(Xt, None, "A"))
    # ---------------- T3 Resolution semantics (D18-8) ----------------
    print("T3  Four cases of the two resolution semantics (D18-8)")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    cases = [("single subthreshold component", [F(1, 20)]), ("several subthreshold components of the same sign", [F(1, 20)] * 3), ("positive and negative components canceling", [F(1, 5), F(-1, 5)])]
    for name, cs in cases:
        host = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda cs_: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p%d" % k, c) for k, c in enumerate(cs_)]}))(cs))
        _, dr, _, rr = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
        _, db, _, rb = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "booked")
        check("T3 %s: original mechanism Aff⁰ ⊆ Act⁰; booked Aff⁰ ⊆ Act^ε" % name, rr["Aff0"] <= rr["Act0"] and rb["Aff0"] <= rb["Acte"])
    host = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p", F(1, 20))]})
    _, _, _, rr = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps)
    check("T3x under the original mechanism Aff⁰ ⊄ Act^ε (single subthreshold component)", rr["Aff0"] - rr["Acte"] == {"x"})
    _, _, _, r0 = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", F(0))
    check("T3w at ε = 0, Act^ε = Act⁰ and Aff^ε = Aff⁰", r0["Acte"] == r0["Act0"] and r0["Affe"] == r0["Aff0"])
    # ---------------- T4 Read-back, Theorem 18-4 ----------------
    print("T4  Read-back, Theorem 18-4: the two readings of the history condition; embedding into the [S2] F layer")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    grid = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(1, 2), F(1)]
    vals = [F(0), F(1, 5), F(1, 2), F(1)]
    incl, strict = True, False
    count = 0
    for k in [F(0), F(1, 20), F(1, 4), F(1), F(-1, 2)]:
        for b in [F(0), F(1, 3)]:
            for s0 in grid:
                for s1 in grid:
                    for law in [("lin", b, k, ("t0",)), ("lin", b, k, ("t0", "t1")), ("bil", b, k, "t0", "t1")]:
                        s = {"t0": s0, "t1": s1}
                        formed = {"t0", "t1"}
                        hf = C.histcond_F(law, s, ["t0", "t1"], eps, formed)
                        hd = C.histcond_diff(law, s, ["t0", "t1"], eps, formed, {"t0": vals, "t1": vals})
                        count += 1
                        if hd == 1 and hf == 0:
                            incl = False
                        if hf == 1 and hd == 0:
                            strict = True
    check("T4a difference reading (numerical part of [17] Definition 6′) ⇒ functional-support reading ([S2] D18): no counterexample in %d grid cases" % count, incl)
    check("T4b the converse fails: there are cases with functional-support reading 1 and difference reading 0 (small coefficient)", strict)
    law = ("lin", F(0), F(1, 20), ("t0",))
    s = {"t0": F(1)}
    check("T4c concrete case: c = (1/20)·d(t0), d(t0) = 1, ε = 1/10: F-layer HistCond = 1, difference reading = 0 (|Δ| ≤ 1/20)",
          C.histcond_F(law, s, ["t0"], eps, {"t0"}) == 1 and C.histcond_diff(law, s, ["t0"], eps, {"t0"}, {"t0": vals}) == 0)
    # Embedding: the original mechanism and booking as effective ([S2] 4.2) give the same trajectory ⇔ at every beat and position the nonzero subthreshold components sum to zero
    ok = True
    for cs in ([F(1, 5)], [F(1, 20)], [F(1, 20), F(-1, 20)], [F(1, 20), F(1, 5)], [F(1, 5), F(-1, 5)], [F(1, 20), F(1, 20), F(-1, 10)]):
        host = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda cs_: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p%d" % k, c) for k, c in enumerate(cs_)]}))(cs))
        _, dr, _, _ = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
        _, db, _, _ = C.step(host, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "booked")
        sub = sum(c for c in cs if 0 < abs(c) <= eps)
        if (dr == db) != (sub == 0):
            ok = False
    check("T4d one beat of the original mechanism equals one beat of booking as effective ⇔ the nonzero subthreshold components sum to zero (six cases)", ok)
    # The fly α3 write (with clipping removed) compiles to the linear law c = −ηa + ηb·Σ_{K(s)} d_γ1
    ok = True
    for v0 in grid:
        for v1 in grid:
            dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(0, "g1")] = v0; dd[(1, "g1")] = v1
            y = M.fly_read(dd, None, "A")
            c_sys = [c for p, c in M.fly_comps(dd, y, "A")[(0, "a3")] if p == "W_a3"][0]
            c_lin = C.f_component(("lin", -ETA_A(), ETA_B(), ((0, "g1"), (1, "g1"))), dd)
            if c_sys != c_lin:
                ok = False
    check("T4e the fly α3 write (activity-mediated branch) with clipping removed compiles to an [S2] linear law: c = −ηa + ηb·Σ_{K(A)} d_γ1 (16 grid cases)", ok)
    y = M.fly_read(M.fly_zero(), None, "A")
    dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(0, "a3")] = F(1)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        dd[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    y = M.fly_read(dd, None, "A")
    _, dn, _, rec = C.step(fly, dd, None, "A")
    raw_c = [c for p, c in rec["comps"][(0, "a3")]][0]
    check("T4f clipping makes post-state ≠ pre-state + component (d(0,a3): 1 + %s → 1); this lies outside the [S2] F-layer law class and must go into the R layer (D9 joint reading, or CLIP listed separately)" % fs(raw_c),
          dn[(0, "a3")] == 1 and raw_c > 0)
    # ---------------- T5 Local loop gain (Theorem 18-5) ----------------
    print("T5  Local bearing of the tendency and loop gain (Theorem 18-5)")
    gam, h = F(11, 50), F(1, 2)
    for name, g, expect in (("R_th = 1/4", F(4), "vanishes"), ("R_th = 4", F(1, 4), "retained")):
        d0, q0 = F(1), F(1, 2)
        I0 = q0 * (g + d0) / d0
        R = 1 / g
        V = I0 / g
        # One-dimensional map d ↦ d + h(f(dV/(1+dR)) − γd): loop gain at d0, L = f′(q)·∂q/∂d
        q = d0 * V / (1 + d0 * R)
        fprime = 2 * q / (1 + q * q) ** 2
        dqdd = V / (1 + d0 * R) ** 2
        Lg = fprime * dqdd
        dd, traj = d0, []
        for _ in range(80):
            qq = dd * V / (1 + dd * R) if dd > 0 else F(0)
            dd = F(round((dd + h * (M.f_tero2(qq) - gam * dd)) * 256), 256) if dd > 0 else F(0)
            dd = dd if dd >= F(1, 32) else F(0)
            traj.append(dd)
        fate = "vanishes" if traj[-1] == 0 else "retained"
        check("T5 %s: loop gain L = f′·∂Q/∂D = %s, %s γ = %s; after 80 beats: %s" % (name, fs(Lg), ">" if Lg > gam else "<", fs(gam), fate),
              fate == expect and ((Lg > gam) == (expect == "vanishes")))
    # Sign law: γ1 self-loop toy
    s_neg, s_pos = F(3, 4), F(3, 4)
    seq_neg, seq_pos = [], []
    for _ in range(30):
        s_neg, _, gd1 = M.loop_step(s_neg, F(-1, 4), c=F(1, 2))
        s_pos, _, gd2 = M.loop_step(s_pos, F(1, 4), c=F(1, 4))
        seq_neg.append(s_neg); seq_pos.append(s_pos)
    check("T5c sign law (positive loop): for u < 0 the deep state stays above 1/2 (continued writing)", all(v > F(1, 2) for v in seq_neg))
    check("T5d sign law (negative loop): for u > 0 the depth falls back and is confined near 1/2 (self-limiting: thereafter ≤ 1/2 + A)",
          min(seq_pos) <= F(1, 2) and all(v <= F(1, 2) + F(1, 4) for v in seq_pos[10:]))
    # ---------------- T6 Equivalent bearing (Theorem 18-6) ----------------
    print("T6  Equivalent bearing: the tendency received by the position itself is affine for linear convergence and linear-fractional for conductance networks")
    ok = True
    for rest in grid:
        dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(1, "a3")] = rest
        ys = [M.fly_read(dict(dd, **{}), None, "A") for _ in [0]]
        vals_m = []
        for own in [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]:
            e = dict(dd); e[(0, "a3")] = own
            vals_m.append(M.fly_read(e, None, "A")["MBON_a3"])
        if not (vals_m[0] - vals_m[1] == vals_m[1] - vals_m[2] == F(1, 2) and vals_m[0] == 2 - rest):
            ok = False
    check("T6a MBON-α3(A) is affine in the own position d(0,a3), (2 − rest) − d_own; the rest of the set bears on it only through the intercept rest (4 grid cases)", ok)
    nodes5 = [0, 1, 2, 3]
    edges5 = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D0 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    k = 4
    Dm = list(D0); Dm[k] = F(0)
    p, _ = M.flows(nodes5, edges5, Dm, 0, 3, 1)
    Vt = p[1] - p[2]
    pr = M.solve(nodes5, edges5, Dm, 1, 2, 1)
    Rt = pr[1] - pr[2]
    ok = True
    for own in [F(1, 4), F(1), F(3)]:
        Dv = list(D0); Dv[k] = own
        _, Q = M.flows(nodes5, edges5, Dv, 0, 3, 1)
        if Q[k] != own * Vt / (1 + own * Rt):
            ok = False
    check("T6b conductance network: flow in tube (1,2) = d·V/(1 + dR), with V = %s, R = %s borne by the rest of the set" % (fs(Vt), fs(Rt)), ok)
    # ---------------- T7 Hidden change (Theorem 18-7) ----------------
    print("T7  Closed-loop criterion for hidden change (Theorem 18-7)")
    check("T7a partial reading: A_cl = [[1,1],[0,1]], C = [1,0], Δ = (0,1): judged manifest",
          not C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1), F(1)], [F(0), F(1)]], [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)]))
    check("T7b parallel opposition: permanently hidden at equal rates, manifest at unequal rates",
          C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(4, 5), 0], [0, F(4, 5)]], [[F(-2), F(2)]], [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]) and
          not C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(19, 20), 0], [0, F(4, 5)]], [[F(-2), F(2)]], [F(1, 2), F(1, 2)]))
    # ---------------- T8 Consolidation state (Theorem 18-8) ----------------
    print("T8  The write law rewritten by history: the consolidation state as a law-layer Trace-Response (Theorem 18-8)")
    flyc = M.make_flyc()
    base = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        base[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    P0 = dict(base); P1 = dict(base)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        P1[("c", i)] = F(1)
    check("T8a same d, different consolidation state c: the current full activities agree under every signal (tendency null space)",
          all(M.flyc_read(P0, None, s) == M.flyc_read(P1, None, s) for s in "ABC"))
    y0, _, _ = C.run(flyc, P0, None, ("R",) * 3 + ("A",))
    y1, _, _ = C.run(flyc, P1, None, ("R",) * 3 + ("A",))
    check("T8b A presented after 3 rest beats: the different retention ratios make MBON-α3(A) differ (manifest through the existing depth): %s vs %s" %
          (fs(y0[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(y1[-1]["MBON_a3"])), y0[-1] != y1[-1])
    Z0 = M.flyc_zero(); Z1 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Z1[("c", i)] = F(1)
    same, n = C.exact_dyn_same(flyc, (Z0, None), (Z1, None), "BR", 5)
    check("T8c at zero depth the consolidation-state difference stays permanently hidden on the %d histories containing only B and rest (no new write lands on these positions)" % n, same)
    G1 = dict(Z0); G2 = dict(Z1)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        G1[(i, "g1")] = G2[(i, "g1")] = F(13, 32)
    ya, da, _ = C.run(flyc, G1, None, ("A", "R", "R", "R", "A"))
    yb, db, _ = C.run(flyc, G2, None, ("A", "R", "R", "R", "A"))
    check("T8d manifest after a weak write below the consolidation threshold: PPL1-α3(A) = 1/16 (> 0 but ≤ θ_c) writes α3 without setting the consolidation state; after rest the readings at the second presentation of A differ: %s vs %s" %
          (fs(ya[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(yb[-1]["MBON_a3"])), ya[-1] != yb[-1] and da[("c", 0)] == 0)
    H1 = dict(Z0); H2 = dict(Z1)
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        H1[(i, "g1")] = H2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    same, n = C.exact_dyn_same(flyc, (H1, None), (H2, None), "ABR", 4)
    check("T8g a strong write (PPL1-α3 > θ_c) also sets the consolidation state and overwrites the earlier consolidation-state difference: the readings agree beat by beat on the %d ABR histories of length ≤ 4 (finite verification; equal complete states after overwriting: see T15d; late manifestation without overwriting: see T15e)" % n, same)
    # History-controlled vs clock-controlled consolidation: discrimination by DAN blockade
    T0 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        T0[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); T0[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    hist = ("A", "R", "R", "R", "R", "A")
    hb_host = M.make_flyc(block_dan=True)
    hb_ys, hb_d, _ = C.run(hb_host, T0, None, hist)
    hn_ys, hn_d, _ = C.run(M.make_flyc(), T0, None, hist)
    cb_ys, cb_d = M.flyc_clock_run(dict(T0), hist, 1, block_dan=True)
    cn_ys, cn_d = M.flyc_clock_run(dict(T0), hist, 1, block_dan=False)
    # Both arms have the same training history and the same blockade; under both hypotheses the blockade removes the direct-write component of α3 (ruling out the explanation “the blockade changes retention only because it removes the direct write”)
    cs_h = hb_host.comps(T0, M.flyc_read(T0, None, "A"), "A")
    cs_c = M.make_flyc(clock=1, block_dan=True).comps(T0, M.flyc_read(T0, None, "A"), "A")
    no_direct = all(p_[0] != "W_a3" for lst in list(cs_h.values()) + list(cs_c.values()) for p_ in lst)
    check("T8e the two arms (history-controlled, clock-controlled) have the same training, the same read-out and the same blockade, and in both arms the blockade removes the α3 direct-write component", no_direct)
~~~
