    check("T8f blockade arms: history-controlled c stays 0, clock-controlled c is set to 1; the two arms differ in the final α3 state and the last-beat reading (the difference passes only through c): MBON-α3(A) %s vs %s" %
          (fs(hb_ys[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(cb_ys[-1]["MBON_a3"])),
          hb_d[("c", 0)] == 0 and cb_d[("c", 0)] == 1 and hb_d[(0, "a3")] < cb_d[(0, "a3")] and hb_ys[-1] != cb_ys[-1])
    check("T8h without blockade: c is set to 1 under both hypotheses (without blockade the two cannot be distinguished)", hn_d[("c", 0)] == cn_d[("c", 0)] == 1)
    # Effective time: c is read from the pre-beat state; a new c written in this beat takes effect from the next beat
    Tq = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Tq[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); Tq[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    _, d1q, _, recq = C.step(M.make_flyc(), Tq, None, "A")
    rel = [c for p_, c in recq["comps"][(0, "a3")] if p_ == "RELAX_a3"][0]
    check("T8i effective time: in the beat that writes c, the α3 relaxation is still computed with the old c (ρ = 1/2): %s; the new c takes effect from the next beat" % fs(rel),
          rel == -(1 - M.RHO[0]) * Tq[(0, "a3")] and d1q[("c", 0)] == 1)
    # A saturating read-out can make the state differences caused by the law layer invisible
    sat = lambda d: min(d, F(1, 2))
    check("T8j the law-layer difference produces depths 1 and 3/4; the saturating read-out min(d, 1/2) gives 1/2 for both states: the state difference is not manifest", sat(F(1)) == sat(F(3, 4)) == F(1, 2))
    # ---------------- T9 Changes in organizational relations (Theorem 18-9) ----------------
    print("T9  Changes in connection, overlap and subset relations (Theorem 18-9)")
    dd = M.fly_zero(); dd[(1, "g1")] = F(1)
    keff = lambda d, s, c: {i for i in M.ODOR[s] if d[(i, c)] < 1}
    ov0 = keff(M.fly_zero(), "A", "g1") & keff(M.fly_zero(), "C", "g1")
    ov1 = keff(dd, "A", "g1") & keff(dd, "C", "g1")
    check("T9a effective overlap is derived from depth: after the γ1 position (1,g1) is depressed to the limit, the effective overlap of A and C in γ1 changes from {1} to ∅", ov0 == {1} and ov1 == set())
    check("T9b a change of overlap changes the tendency: MBON-γ1 at C changes from 2 to 1",
          M.fly_read(M.fly_zero(), None, "C")["MBON_g1"] == 2 and M.fly_read(dd, None, "C")["MBON_g1"] == 1)
    nodes4 = [0, 1, 2, 3]
    edges4 = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D4 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    t4 = M.make_tero(nodes4, edges4, 0, 3, F(1), F(1, 2), F(1), F(0))
    d4 = {k: D4[k] for k in range(5)}
    d4c = dict(d4); d4c[0] = F(0)
    y4, y4c = t4.read(d4, None, "I"), t4.read(d4c, None, "I")
    check("T9c conductance network: once a tube segment reaches the shallowing limit (conductance 0) the derived incidence changes; flow in the non-adjacent tube (2,3) %s → %s" %
          (fs(y4["Q"][3]), fs(y4c["Q"][3])), y4["Q"][3] != y4c["Q"][3])
    ch = M.make_chain()
    dch = M.chain_zero()
    hist_present = []
    for t in range(7):
        y, dch, _, rec = C.step(ch, dch, None, "S")
        hist_present.append(sum(1 for x in range(4) if dch[("e", x)] == 1))
    check("T9d minimal representation of Edge-Seepage: once the first position is full and the signal continues, new positions are generated along the edge; number of existing positions %s" % hist_present,
          hist_present[0] == 1 and hist_present[-1] > 1 and all(hist_present[i] <= hist_present[i + 1] for i in range(6)))
    check("T9e the tendency changes after generation: the passage target of the same signal S changes from “no position to enter” to the new position",
          ch.read(dch, None, "S")["tgt"] is not None)
    # ---------------- T10 Read-back: path origins, read certification, joint contrasts and F-layer booking ----------------
    print("T10  Read-back: path origins, read certification, joint contrasts and F-layer booking (Theorem 18-4(2)(4))")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    law = ("lin", F(1), F(1, 20), ("d",))
    s1 = {"d": F(1)}
    ok_path = lambda S: 1
    check("T10a separation of the readings on an effective write: c = 1 + d/20, d = 1, ε = 1/10: actual component 21/20 > ε; functional support (path from Avail, read certification 1) established; maximal joint-contrast difference 1/20, numeric difference does not cross threshold",
          C.f_component(law, s1) == F(21, 20) and C.histcond_F_full(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, ok_path) == 1 and
          C.histcond_diff_joint(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, {"d": [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]}) == "numeric difference does not cross threshold")
    only_other = lambda S: 1 if S == frozenset({"z"}) else 0
    check("T10b the path must start from the true Avail_act origin set: with only a path from the unavailable position z, HistCond_F = 0",
          C.histcond_F_full(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, only_other) == 0)
    check("T10c with read certification ⊥, HistCond_F = ⊥ (not raised to 1)", C.histcond_F_full(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, ok_path, "⊥") == "⊥")
    law6 = ("lin", F(1), F(3, 50), ("a", "b"))
    st6 = {"a": F(1), "b": F(1)}
    check("T10d joint contrast: the single-set subprotocol has no witness (3/50 each); the joint setting gives a difference 3/25 > ε: numeric difference crosses threshold",
          C.histcond_diff(law6, st6, ["a", "b"], eps, {"a", "b"}, {"a": [F(0)], "b": [F(0)]}) == 0 and
          C.histcond_diff_joint(law6, st6, ["a", "b"], eps, {"a", "b"}, {"a": [F(0)], "b": [F(0)]}) == "numeric difference crosses threshold")
    r_tri = C.readback_hist(law, s1, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, {"d": [F(0), F(1)]}, ok_path)
    check("T10e the two columns of raw judgments: %s / %s" % r_tri, r_tri == ("functional support established", "numeric difference does not cross threshold"))
    cases_ce = [[(F(1), 0)], [(F(1), 1)], [(F(1, 20), 1), (F(-1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 5), 1), (F(1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 5), 0), (F(1, 5), 1)]]
    gen_ok = all((C.raw_update(cs) == C.f_layer_update(cs, eps)) ==
                 (sum(c for c, _ in cs) == sum(c for c, ce in cs if abs(c) > eps and ce == 1)) for cs in cases_ce)
    check("T10f F-layer booking counts cert_eff: with a constant component +1 and cert_eff = 0 the subthreshold sum is zero, yet the original mechanism and the F layer differ",
          C.raw_update(cases_ce[0]) != C.f_layer_update(cases_ce[0], eps))
    check("T10g the general condition “equal booking totals on both sides from the same pre-state” is equivalent to one-beat equality (five cases)", gen_ok)
    print("T11  |F′| = 1 and the two interventions (Theorem 18-5(2)(5))")
    def iterate(fn, u, n):
        out = [u]
        for _ in range(n):
            u = fn(u); out.append(u)
        return out
    a1 = iterate(lambda u: u - u ** 3, F(1, 4), 5)
    a2 = iterate(lambda u: u + u ** 3, F(1, 4), 5)
    check("T11a two examples with F′ = 1: starting from 1/4, u ↦ u − u³ strictly decreases in absolute value and u ↦ u + u³ strictly increases: at |F′| = 1 higher orders decide",
          all(abs(a1[k + 1]) < abs(a1[k]) for k in range(5)) and all(abs(a2[k + 1]) > abs(a2[k]) for k in range(5)))
    phi_U = lambda dV: 2 - dV
    wU = lambda dU, y: dU + F(7)
    check("T11b nonzero activity intervention but zero post-state intervention: ∂Φ_U/∂d_V = −1, the write is constant in the activity, J_UV = 0",
          phi_U(F(1)) != phi_U(F(0)) and wU(F(1, 2), phi_U(F(1))) == wU(F(1, 2), phi_U(F(0))))
    print("T12  Remote effects of removal (Theorem 18-9(2)(A))")
    nodes4 = [0, 1, 2, 3]
    edges4 = [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D4 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    _, Qo = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, D4, 0, 3, 1)
    Dc = list(D4); Dc[0] = F(0)
    _, Qn = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, Dc, 0, 3, 1)
    pT = M.solve(nodes4, edges4, Dc, 0, 1, 1)
    Tx = [dd_ * (pT[u] - pT[v]) if dd_ > 0 else F(0) for (u, v), dd_ in zip(edges4, Dc)]
    check("T12a the flow change caused by removing the tube segment e = (0,1) is ΔQ_x = Q_e·T_x(u→v) (T is the flow in x when, with e removed, a unit flow is injected at u and withdrawn at v); holds for each of the other four tubes",
          all(Qn[k] - Qo[k] == Qo[0] * Tx[k] for k in range(1, 5)))
    Db = [F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1)]
    _, Qb = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, Db, 0, 3, 1)
    Dbc = list(Db); Dbc[4] = F(0)
    _, Qbc = M.flows(nodes4, edges4, Dbc, 0, 3, 1)
    check("T12b network condition: the bridge tube of a balanced bridge carries flow 0, and removing it changes no remote flow (removal by itself does not imply a remote change)",
          Qb[4] == 0 and Qb[:4] == Qbc[:4])
    print("T13  Source–identity records (D18-2, D18-5)")
    def run_rec(d, hist, g=None):
        rec = []
        for t, sym in enumerate(hist):
            _, d, _, rr = C.step(fly, d, None, sym)
            for x, lst in rr["comps"].items():
                for pid, c in lst:
                    if c != 0 and pid == "W_a3":
                        rec.append((t, x, pid))
        return d, rec
    Xa = M.fly_zero(); Xa[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 8)
    Xb = M.fly_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Xb[(i, "g1")] = F(5, 8)
    dA, recA = run_rec(dict(Xa), ())
    pre = M.fly_zero(); pre[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 8)
    check("T13a equal complete mathematical states (same d, r) can come from different formation records: a declared initial value and formation by writing must be told apart by the record, and the record is not inferred from the numerical state",
          dA == pre and recA == [])
    pi = {0: 3, 1: 2, 2: 1, 3: 0}; sig = {"A": "B", "B": "A", "C": "C", "R": "R"}
    h0 = ("A", "R", "C", "A")
    _, r1 = run_rec(dict(Xb), h0)
    _, r2 = run_rec({(pi[i], c): v for (i, c), v in Xb.items()}, tuple(sig[x] for x in h0))
    check("T13b formation records commute with relabeling: after the labels are changed by π the record entries agree one by one (one case)",
          sorted((t, (pi[i], c), p_) for t, (i, c), p_ in r1) == sorted(r2))
    # ---------------- T14 Actual equation, common domain, residue projection and three-valued conjunction ----------------
    print("T14  Actual equation, common domain, residue projection and the D18 three-valued conjunction (Theorem 18-4(2)(4))")
    eps = F(1, 10)
    # Comparison of the actual equation post(d + Σorig) with d + ΣF; common domain
    cap_post = lambda x: min(x, F(1))
    cap = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p", F(1))]},
                 lambda dn, do: {"x": cap_post(dn["x"])})
    _, after_raw, _, _ = C.step(cap, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
    f_after = F(1) + C.f_layer_update([(F(1), 1)], eps)
    check("T14a initial state 1, both sides book +1 (equal totals): original mechanism post(1 + 1) = %s, F layer 1 + 1 = %s; the post-states differ, so equal booking totals do not suffice for one-beat equality" %
          (fs(after_raw["x"]), fs(f_after)),
          C.raw_update([(F(1), 1)]) == C.f_layer_update([(F(1), 1)], eps) and after_raw["x"] == 1 and f_after == 2
          and not C.f_post_match(cap_post, F(1), F(1), F(1)))
    ok_eq, ok_dom, n_case = True, True, 0
    for d0 in [F(0), F(1, 4), F(3, 4), F(1)]:
        for cs in ([(F(1, 5), 1)], [(F(1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 20), 1), (F(-1, 20), 1)], [(F(1, 2), 1), (F(1, 20), 1)],
                   [(F(1, 5), 0)], [(F(1, 2), 1), (F(1, 2), 1)]):
            n_case += 1
            raw_sum = C.raw_update(cs); f_sum = C.f_layer_update(cs, eps)
            h = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda cs_: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p%d" % k, c) for k, (c, _) in enumerate(cs_)]}))(cs),
                       lambda dn, do: {"x": cap_post(dn["x"])})
            _, dr, _, _ = C.step(h, {"x": d0}, None, "s", eps, "raw")
            same = dr["x"] == d0 + f_sum
            if same != C.f_post_match(cap_post, d0, raw_sum, f_sum):
                ok_eq = False
            if cap_post(d0 + raw_sum) == d0 + raw_sum and cap_post(d0 + f_sum) == d0 + f_sum:
                if same != (raw_sum == f_sum):
                    ok_dom = False
    check("T14b the actual-equation criterion post(d + Σorig) = d + ΣF is equivalent to one-beat equality (%d cases, clipping at upper limit 1)" % n_case, ok_eq)
    check("T14c on the common domain (post triggers on neither side in that beat), one-beat equality ⟺ equal booking totals (those of the same %d cases that fall in this domain)" % n_case, ok_dom)
    rd = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {"y": r}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("W_r", y["y"])]}, None, lambda r, y: r)
    tr0 = [dd_["x"] for dd_ in [C.run(rd, {"x": F(0)}, F(0), ("s",))[1]]]
    tr1 = [dd_["x"] for dd_ in [C.run(rd, {"x": F(0)}, F(1), ("s",))[1]]]
    check("T14d with residue: the complete states (d, r) = (0, 0) and (0, 1) have the same d projection; the write reads the residue, and after one beat d is %s vs %s; when F carries only d, trajectory equality compares only projections, so a projection or a faithful augmentation must be declared" %
          (fs(tr0[0]), fs(tr1[0])), tr0[0] != tr1[0])
    # Full D18 conjunction; 0 takes precedence over ⊥
    law = ("lin", F(1), F(1), ("x",))
    vals3 = [0, 1, "⊥"]
    ok_tt = True
    for av in (0, 1):
        sx = {"x": F(1) if av else F(0)}
        for cr in vals3:
            for pa in vals3:
                got = C.histcond_F_full(law, sx, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, (lambda p_: (lambda _S: p_))(pa), cert_read=cr)
                if got != C.kleene_and([av, cr, pa if av else 0]):
                    ok_tt = False
    check("T14e HistCond_F is the three-valued conjunction [Avail≠∅] ∧ cert_read ∧ Path: each of the 18 combinations agrees with “any term 0 gives 0; no 0 but some ⊥ gives ⊥”; with read certification ⊥ and path 0 it is 0",
          ok_tt and C.histcond_F_full(law, {"x": F(1)}, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, lambda _: 0, cert_read="⊥") == 0)
    lawA = ("lin", F(1), F(1, 20), ("d",))
    atomsA, colA = C.readback_atoms(lawA, {"d": F(1)}, ["d"], eps, {"d"}, {"d": [F(0), F(1, 2), F(1)]}, lambda _: 1)
    check("T14f the three columns are a summary and the atoms are kept: in the case of T10a the atoms are Avail = %s, cert_read = %s, Path = %s, HistCond_F = %s, Act_F = %s, numeric difference “%s”, summary “%s”; an uncertified difference does not override HistCond_F = 1" %
          (atomsA["Avail"], atomsA["cert_read"], atomsA["Path"], atomsA["HistCond_F"], atomsA["Act_F"], atomsA["numeric difference"], colA),
          atomsA["HistCond_F"] == 1 and atomsA["numeric difference"] == "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold" and colA == "functional support established")
    lawB = ("bil", F(0), F(1), "j1", "j2")
    sB = {"j1": F(1), "j2": F(0)}
    s2B = dict(sB); s2B["j2"] = F(1)
    big = abs(C.f_component(lawB, s2B) - C.f_component(lawB, sB))
    atomsB, colB = C.readback_atoms(lawB, sB, ["j1", "j2"], eps, {"j1", "j2"}, {"j1": [F(0)], "j2": [F(1)]}, lambda _: 1)
    check("T14g a nonzero finite difference is not nonzero functional support at the actual state: c = s(j1)s(j2), s = (1, 0); setting j2, whose depth is 0, to 1 gives a difference %s > ε (this contrast does not satisfy clause (B) of Definition 6′), and the partner of j1 is 0: HistCond_F = %s, numeric difference “%s”, summary “%s”" %
          (fs(big), atomsB["HistCond_F"], atomsB["numeric difference"], colB),
          big > eps and atomsB["HistCond_F"] == 0 and atomsB["numeric difference"] == "numeric difference zero" and colB == "effective difference not certified")
    print("T15  First component difference, actual post-state difference and overwriting (Theorem 18-8(2)(4))")
    flyc = M.make_flyc()
    a0 = M.flyc_zero(); a0[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 10)
    a1 = dict(a0); a1[("c", 0)] = F(1)
    ya0, da0, _, ra0 = C.step(flyc, a0, None, "A")
    ya1, da1, _, ra1 = C.step(flyc, a1, None, "A")
    pre0 = a0[(0, "a3")] + sum(c for _, c in ra0["comps"][(0, "a3")])
    pre1 = a1[(0, "a3")] + sum(c for _, c in ra1["comps"][(0, "a3")])
    check("T15a FLYC: γ1 at 0, α3 depth 1/10, c = 0 and 1, A presented: the first component difference appears in this beat (relaxation %s vs %s); before clipping %s vs %s, after clipping both 0: the first component difference is not the first actual post-state difference" %
          (fs([c for p_, c in ra0["comps"][(0, "a3")] if p_ == "RELAX_a3"][0]), fs([c for p_, c in ra1["comps"][(0, "a3")] if p_ == "RELAX_a3"][0]),
           fs(pre0), fs(pre1)),
          ra0["comps"][(0, "a3")] != ra1["comps"][(0, "a3")] and pre0 == F(-11, 80) and pre1 == F(-39, 400)
          and da0[(0, "a3")] == da1[(0, "a3")] == 0 and all(da0[p_] == da1[p_] for p_ in M.FLY_POS))
    _, dbk0, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a0, None, "R", eps, "booked")
    _, dbk1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a1, None, "R", eps, "booked")
    _, drw0, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a0, None, "R", eps, "raw")
    _, drw1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, a1, None, "R", eps, "raw")
    check("T15b booking as effective can discard a component difference: depth 1/10 at rest, the relaxations −1/20 and −1/100 are both ≤ ε = 1/10, and under booked-as-effective semantics both arms have post-state %s; under the original mechanism %s vs %s" %
          (fs(dbk0[(0, "a3")]), fs(drw0[(0, "a3")]), fs(drw1[(0, "a3")])),
          dbk0[(0, "a3")] == dbk1[(0, "a3")] == F(1, 10) and drw0[(0, "a3")] != drw1[(0, "a3")])
    b0 = M.flyc_zero(); b0[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    b1 = dict(b0); b1[("c", 0)] = F(1)
    _, db0, _, _ = C.step(flyc, b0, None, "R")
    _, db1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, b1, None, "R")
    check("T15c with neither arm truncated and booking under the original mechanism, the actual post-state difference equals the component difference (ρ(c′) − ρ(c))·d_x: %s − %s = %s" %
          (fs(db1[(0, "a3")]), fs(db0[(0, "a3")]), fs(db1[(0, "a3")] - db0[(0, "a3")])),
          db1[(0, "a3")] - db0[(0, "a3")] == (M.RHO[1] - M.RHO[0]) * b0[(0, "a3")])
    # Overwriting requires equal complete mathematical states; a hidden difference that is not overwritten can manifest late; strong training does not erase a difference already produced
    H1 = M.flyc_zero(); H2 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        H1[(i, "g1")] = H2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); H2[("c", i)] = F(1)
    _, h1n, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H1, None, "A")
    _, h2n, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H2, None, "A")
    check("T15d overwriting: after the strong-write beat the complete mathematical states of the two runs (d and c at every position; no residue, no clock-controlled timer, no existence variables) agree position by position, hence beat by beat thereafter", h1n == h2n)
    late = ("R",) * 6 + ("A", "R", "A")
    yl1, dl1, _ = C.run(flyc, H1, None, late)
    yl2, dl2, _ = C.run(flyc, H2, None, late)
    first = next((k for k in range(len(late)) if yl1[k] != yl2[k]), None)
    ppl = yl1[6]["PPL1_a3"]
    check("T15e without overwriting, “no readable difference so far” does not imply permanent hiding: for the same pair of initial states, after 6 rest beats PPL1-α3(A) = %s (> 0 but ≤ θ_c, a weak write), and the readings first separate at beat %d (MBON-α3(A) %s vs %s); T8g, with length ≤ 4, does not cover this history" %
          (fs(ppl), (first or 0) + 1, fs(yl1[-1]["MBON_a3"]), fs(yl2[-1]["MBON_a3"])),
          0 < ppl <= M.THETA_C and first == len(late) - 1 and all(yl1[k] == yl2[k] for k in range(len(late) - 1)))
    E1 = M.flyc_zero(); E2 = M.flyc_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        E1[(i, "g1")] = E2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4); E1[(i, "a3")] = E2[(i, "a3")] = F(1, 2); E2[("c", i)] = F(1)
    ye1, de1, _ = C.run(flyc, E1, None, ("R", "A", "A"))
    ye2, de2, _ = C.run(flyc, E2, None, ("R", "A", "A"))
    check("T15f strong training does not erase a state difference already produced: at depth 1/2 one rest beat already produces a difference; after the subsequent strong writes c is set to 1 in both arms, yet the α3 depths are still %s vs %s and the readings still differ" %
          (fs(de1[(0, "a3")]), fs(de2[(0, "a3")])),
          de1[("c", 0)] == de2[("c", 0)] == 1 and de1[(0, "a3")] != de2[(0, "a3")] and ye1[-1] != ye2[-1])
    print("T16  Boundary conditions and solvability (Theorem 18-9(2)(B)(C))")
    n2, e2, D2 = [0, 1], [(0, 1), (0, 1)], [F(1), F(1)]
    q_old = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, D2, {0: 1, 1: 0}, {})
    q_new = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, [F(0), F(1)], {0: 1, 1: 0}, {})
    T_inj = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, [F(0), F(1)], {1: 0}, {0: 1})
    T0 = M.removal_response(n2, e2, D2, {0: 1, 1: 0}, 0)
    check("T16a ends held at fixed potentials 1 and 0, two parallel branches of conductance 1 each: removing one branch, the flow in the other goes %s → %s; applying the fixed-injection transfer T = %s predicts a change of +%s (mismatch); the incremental response under the same fixed-potential boundary, T⁰ = %s, predicts 0 (match)" %
          (fs(q_old[1]), fs(q_new[1]), fs(T_inj[1]), fs(q_old[0] * T_inj[1]), fs(T0[1])),
          q_new[1] - q_old[1] == 0 and q_old[0] * T_inj[1] == 1 and q_old[0] * T0[1] == q_new[1] - q_old[1])
    qi_old = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, D2, {1: 0}, {0: 2})
    qi_new = M.flows_bc(n2, e2, [F(0), F(1)], {1: 0}, {0: 2})
    check("T16b the same network with a fixed injection of 2 instead: removing one branch, the other goes %s → %s, ΔQ = Q_e·T(u→v) = %s" % (fs(qi_old[1]), fs(qi_new[1]), fs(qi_old[0] * T_inj[1])),
          qi_new[1] - qi_old[1] == qi_old[0] * T_inj[1])
    n4, e4 = [0, 1, 2, 3], [(0, 1), (0, 2), (1, 3), (2, 3), (1, 2)]
    D4 = [F(1), F(1, 2), F(1, 4), F(1), F(1)]
    ok_bc, n_bc = True, 0
    for fixed, inj in (({0: 1, 3: 0}, {}), ({0: 1, 3: 0}, {2: F(1, 2)}), ({3: 0}, {0: F(1)}), ({3: 0}, {0: F(1), 2: F(-1, 3)})):
        q0 = M.flows_bc(n4, e4, D4, fixed, inj)
        for k in range(5):
            Dk = list(D4); Dk[k] = F(0)
            qk = M.flows_bc(n4, e4, Dk, fixed, inj)
            Tk = M.removal_response(n4, e4, D4, fixed, k)
            n_bc += 1
            if any(qk[x] - q0[x] != q0[k] * Tk[x] for x in range(5) if x != k):
                ok_bc = False
    check("T16c the incremental-response formula ΔQ_x = Q_e·T⁰_x(u→v) (T⁰: same boundary, zero increment at fixed-potential ends) holds tube by tube on the Wheatstone network: four boundaries of fixed-potential, mixed and fixed-injection type × five removed edges = %d cases" % n_bc, ok_bc)
    n5, e5, D5 = [0, 1, 2, 4], [(0, 1), (0, 1), (1, 2), (2, 4)], [F(1), F(1), F(1), F(1)]
    q5 = M.flows_bc(n5, e5, D5, {1: 0}, {0: 1})
    D5c = list(D5); D5c[3] = F(0)
    q5c = M.flows_bc(n5, e5, D5c, {1: 0}, {0: 1})
    try:
        M.removal_response(n5, e5, D5, {1: 0}, 3); solv = True
    except ValueError:
        solv = False
    check("T16d bridge disconnection (dangling branch): Q_e = %s, and all flows are unchanged after removal; once it is removed u and v are disconnected, the unit-injection problem has no solution, and the removal formula is not invoked" % fs(q5[3]),
          q5[3] == 0 and q5c[:3] == q5[:3] and not solv)
    n6, e6, D6 = [0, 1, 3], [(0, 1), (1, 3)], [F(1), F(1)]
    try:
        M.flows_bc(n6, e6, [F(0), F(1)], {3: 0}, {0: 1}); cons = True
    except ValueError:
        cons = False
    check("T16e bridge disconnection (between source and sink): after removing (0,1), the source's component {0} receives injection 1 but has no fixed-potential node; component conservation fails, the steady state has no solution, and the removal formula is not invoked", not cons)
    # ---------------- T17 Numeric difference and the Definition 6′ complete certificate ----------------
    print("T17  Numeric difference and the [17] Definition 6′ complete certificate (Theorem 18-4, preamble)")
    lawU = ("lin", F(1), F(1), ("x",))
    sU = {"x": F(1)}
    cU = {"x": [F(0)]}
    def rb(path_value, eff, ev=None):
        return C.readback_atoms(lawU, sU, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: path_value, cert_eff=eff, evidence=ev)
    a1, c1 = rb(1, 0)
    check("T17a (no action) c = 1 + x, x = 1 → 0, numeric difference 1: Path = 1, cert_eff = 0: numeric difference “%s”, Act_F = %s, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”; “effective difference certified” is not given" %
          (a1["numeric difference"], a1["Act_F"], a1["complete certificate"], c1),
          a1["numeric difference"] == "numeric difference crosses threshold" and a1["Act_F"] == 0 and a1["complete certificate"] == 0 and c1 == "functional support established" and a1["HistCond_F"] == 1)
    a2, c2 = rb(0, 1)
    check("T17b (route missing) Path = 0, cert_eff = 1: route recorded as %s (path 0 does not exclude another material route, nor is the route inferred back from the numeric difference), complete certificate %s, summary “%s”; numeric difference “%s” kept" %
          (a2["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["route"], a2["complete certificate"], c2, a2["numeric difference"]),
          a2["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["route"] == "⊥" and a2["complete certificate"] == "⊥" and c2 == "effective difference not certified" and a2["numeric difference"] == "numeric difference crosses threshold" and a2["HistCond_F"] == 0)
    a3, c3 = rb("⊥", "⊥")
    check("T17c (certification unknown) Path = ⊥, cert_eff = ⊥: Act_F = %s, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”; the numeric evidence and the unknowns are kept" % (a3["Act_F"], a3["complete certificate"], c3),
          a3["Act_F"] == "⊥" and a3["complete certificate"] == "⊥" and c3 == "effective difference not certified" and a3["HistCond_F"] == "⊥" and a3["numeric difference"] == "numeric difference crosses threshold")
    a4, c4 = rb(1, 1)
    check("T17d (all antecedents hold) Path = 1, cert_eff = 1, same-beat and bearing position given by construction: four clauses %s, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”" %
          (", ".join("%s %s" % kv for kv in a4["Def. 6′ four clauses"].items()), a4["complete certificate"], c4),
          a4["complete certificate"] == 1 and c4 == "effective difference certified")
    a5, c5 = rb(0, 1, {"route": 1})
    a6, c6 = rb(1, 1, {"same-beat": "⊥"})
    a7, c7 = rb(1, 1, {"bearing position": 0})
    check("T17e explicit evidence interface: with Path = 0 but a separately declared valid route carrying its own certificate (route = 1), complete certificate %s, summary “%s”, and HistCond_F is still %s (the original [S2] formula is unchanged); with same-beat certification unknown: %s; with the bearing position failing: %s" %
          (a5["complete certificate"], c5, a5["HistCond_F"], c6, c7),
          a5["complete certificate"] == 1 and c5 == "effective difference certified" and a5["HistCond_F"] == 0 and c6 == "functional support established" and a6["complete certificate"] == "⊥"
          and c7 == "functional support established" and a7["complete certificate"] == 0)
    tt_ok = True
    for av in (0, 1):
        for cr in (0, 1, "⊥"):
            for pa in (0, 1, "⊥"):
                sx = {"x": F(1) if av else F(0)}
                pf = (lambda p_: (lambda _S: p_))(pa)
                if C.histcond_F_full(lawU, sx, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, pf, cr) != C.kleene_and([av, cr, pa if av else 0]):
                    tt_ok = False
    check("T17f the original [S2] D18 conjunction and the read-back under the original conditions are kept: histcond_F_full equals the three-valued conjunction on all 18 combinations; readback_hist returns the two columns of raw judgments (functional support established / numeric difference crosses threshold) and gives no complete certificate",
          tt_ok and C.readback_hist(lawU, sU, ["x"], eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: 1) == ("functional support established", "numeric difference crosses threshold"))
    # ---------------- T18 Merging of action evidence ----------------
    print("T18  Merging of action evidence: refutation, unproven, conflict and independent witness (Theorem 18-4, preamble)")
    v1, cv1 = rb(1, 0, {"action": 1})
    check("T18a (an added action claim does not override a refutation) c = 1 + x, x set from 1 to 0, Path = 1, cert_eff = 0, added evidence = {action: 1}: Act_F = %s, action clause %s, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”, conflict record “%s”" %
          (v1["Act_F"], v1["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["action"], v1["complete certificate"], cv1, "; ".join(v1["evidence conflict"])),
          v1["Act_F"] == 0 and v1["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["action"] == 0 and v1["complete certificate"] == 0 and cv1 != "effective difference certified"
          and len(v1["evidence conflict"]) == 1)
    v2, cv2 = rb(1, 0)
    check("T18b (refutation) the same case without an external claim: action source “%s”, complete certificate %s, no conflict record, summary “%s”" % (v2["action source"], v2["complete certificate"], cv2),
          v2["action source"] == "F-atom refutation" and v2["complete certificate"] == 0 and v2["evidence conflict"] == [] and cv2 == "functional support established")
    v3, cv3 = rb(1, "⊥")
    check("T18c (unproven) cert_eff = ⊥, no external certification: action clause %s, source “%s”, complete certificate %s, no conflict, summary “%s”" % (v3["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["action"], v3["action source"], v3["complete certificate"], cv3),
          v3["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["action"] == "⊥" and v3["action source"] == "unproven" and v3["complete certificate"] == "⊥" and v3["evidence conflict"] == [] and cv3 == "functional support established")
    v4, cv4 = rb(1, "⊥", {"action": 1})
    check("T18d (unproven, supplied by explicit certification) cert_eff = ⊥, evidence = {action: 1}: source “%s”, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”; the Act_F atom is still %s" % (v4["action source"], v4["complete certificate"], cv4, v4["Act_F"]),
          v4["action source"] == "explicit certification" and v4["complete certificate"] == 1 and cv4 == "effective difference certified" and v4["Act_F"] == "⊥")
    v5, cv5 = rb(1, 1, {"action": 0})
    check("T18e (conflict in the other direction) Act_F = 1 but external claim 0: conflict recorded, action clause 0, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”" % (v5["complete certificate"], cv5),
          len(v5["evidence conflict"]) == 1 and v5["complete certificate"] == 0 and cv5 == "functional support established")
    v6, cv6 = rb(1, 1)
    v6b, cv6b = rb(1, 1, {"action": 1})
    check("T18f (complete positive case) Path = 1, cert_eff = 1: complete certificate %s, summary “%s”; the same with a consistent external claim (action 1), no conflict" % (v6["complete certificate"], cv6),
          v6["complete certificate"] == 1 and cv6 == "effective difference certified" and v6b["complete certificate"] == 1 and v6b["evidence conflict"] == [] and cv6b == cv6)
    v7, cv7 = rb(0, 1, {"route": 1})
    check("T18g (explicit route certification supplies the uncertified F path; original design kept) Path = 0, evidence = {route: 1}: complete certificate %s, summary “%s”, HistCond_F = %s" % (v7["complete certificate"], cv7, v7["HistCond_F"]),
          v7["complete certificate"] == 1 and cv7 == "effective difference certified" and v7["HistCond_F"] == 0 and v7["evidence conflict"] == [])
    v9, cv9 = rb(1, "⊥", {"action": "⊥"})
    check("T18h (explicitly unproven) cert_eff = ⊥, evidence = {action: ⊥}: source “%s” (not marked “explicit certification”), action clause %s, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”; the three-valued verdict and the summary are the same as in T18c" %
          (v9["action source"], v9["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["action"], v9["complete certificate"], cv9),
          v9["action source"] == "explicitly unproven" and v9["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["action"] == "⊥" and v9["complete certificate"] == v3["complete certificate"] == "⊥" and cv9 == cv3 and v9["evidence conflict"] == [])
    indep = {"event identity": "same-beat event of another mechanism ρ′", "mode": "R layer", "certification basis": "declared independent route and action evidence", "action": 1}
    v8, cv8 = C.readback_atoms(lawU, sU, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: 1, cert_eff=0, independent=indep)
    v8b, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawU, sU, {"x"}, eps, {"x"}, cU, lambda _: 1, cert_eff=0, independent={"action": 1})
    check("T18i (independent witness listed separately) cert_eff = 0 with an R-layer witness of a different mechanism attached: the witness is listed separately by event identity, mode and certification basis; the complete certificate of this F event is still %s, summary “%s”; with incomplete fields it is recorded as “%s”" %
          (v8["complete certificate"], cv8, v8b["independent witness"]),
          v8["independent witness"] == indep and v8["complete certificate"] == 0 and cv8 == "functional support established" and v8b["independent witness"] == "independent witness incomplete")
    # ---------------- T19 First-arrival intervals of the representative fly model (Section 11) ----------------
    print("T19  Representative fly model: first-arrival intervals of the re-exposure reversal latency (Section 11, PN-1)")
    def nstar(D):
        n_ = 0
        while -M.A_INN + 2 * M.B_DIS * D * M.R1 ** n_ > 0:
            n_ += 1
        return n_
    def interval(n_):
        lo = M.A_INN / (2 * M.B_DIS * M.R1 ** (n_ - 1)) if n_ >= 1 else F(0)
        return lo, M.A_INN / (2 * M.B_DIS * M.R1 ** n_)
    check("T19a same plateau: D = 1/2 and 51/100 both reverse at beat 3", nstar(F(1, 2)) == nstar(F(51, 100)) == 3)
    check("T19b the interval formula a/(2b r1^(n−1)) < D ≤ a/(2b r1^n) agrees with n*(D) pointwise on D = 0.40..1.00 (step 1/200)",
          all(interval(nstar(D))[0] < D <= interval(nstar(D))[1] for D in [F(k, 200) for k in range(80, 201)]))
    (l3, h3), (l9, h9) = interval(3), interval(9)
    m3, m9 = (l3 + h3) / 2, (l9 + h9) / 2
    dlt = min((h3 - l3) / 2, (h9 - l9) / 2) * F(9, 10)
    check("T19c midpoints of the n = 3 and n = 9 intervals %s, %s: within an error δ = %s they keep first-arrival beats 3 and 9 respectively (endpoints verified exactly)" % (fs(m3), fs(m9), fs(dlt)),
          nstar(m3 - dlt) == nstar(m3 + dlt) == 3 and nstar(m9 - dlt) == nstar(m9 + dlt) == 9)
    y_f = M.fly_read(M.fly_zero(), None, "A")
    probe_ok = True
    for k in range(0, 13):
        dd_ = M.fly_zero()
        for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
            dd_[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4) * M.R1 ** k
        dd_[(0, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
        y_ = M.fly_read(dd_, None, "A")
        c_ = [c for p_, c in M.fly_comps(dd_, y_, "A")[(0, "a3")] if p_ == "W_a3"][0]
        if (c_ > 0) != (-M.A_INN + 2 * M.B_DIS * F(3, 4) * M.R1 ** k > 0):
            probe_ok = False
    check("T19d the sign of the α3 write on re-exposure is set by the γ1 depth via PPL1-α3 (the value of the tendency at the write position): for k = 0..12 it has the sign of −a + 2bD·r1^k at every beat", probe_ok)
    # ---------------- T20 Relabeling equivariance under booking as effective (Theorem 18-2(1)) ----------------
    print("T20  Relabeling equivariance under booking as effective: commutation of the filtered write needs a separate proof (Theorem 18-2(1))")
    eb = F(1, 10)
    sw = {"x": "z", "z": "x"}
    hs = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: dict(d),
                lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("A", F(3, 20)), ("B", F(1, 20))], "z": [("A", F(1, 5)), ("B", F(0))]})
    d00 = {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}
    _, raw00, _, _ = C.step(hs, d00, None, "R", eb, "raw")
    _, bk00, _, _ = C.step(hs, d00, None, "R", eb, "booked")
    cs00 = hs.comps(d00, hs.read(d00, None, "R"), "R")
    check("T20a the total write commutes with relabeling (both positions have total write %s) but the filtered write does not: from the symmetric initial state (0, 0) the original-mechanism post-state (%s, %s) is fixed by the swap, the booked-as-effective post-state (%s, %s) is not; the process-pairing condition fails" %
          (fs(raw00["x"]), fs(raw00["x"]), fs(raw00["z"]), fs(bk00["x"]), fs(bk00["z"])),
          raw00["x"] == raw00["z"] and bk00["x"] != bk00["z"] and not C.pairing_ok(cs00, cs00, sw)
          and sum(c for _, c in cs00["x"]) == sum(c for _, c in cs00["z"]))
    hs2 = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: dict(d),
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("A", F(3, 20)), ("B", F(1, 20))], "z": [("A", F(3, 20)), ("B", F(1, 20))]})
    cs2 = hs2.comps(d00, hs2.read(d00, None, "R"), "R")
    _, bk2, _, _ = C.step(hs2, d00, None, "R", eb, "booked")
    check("T20b when the process pairing preserves values component by component (both positions have components (3/20, 1/20)), the booked-as-effective post-state (%s, %s) is fixed by the swap" % (fs(bk2["x"]), fs(bk2["z"])),
          C.pairing_ok(cs2, cs2, sw) and bk2["x"] == bk2["z"])
    ok_pair, ok_eq = True, True
    for pi, sig, tau in G4:
        pmap = {(i, c): (pi[i], tau[c]) for (i, c) in M.FLY_POS}
        for L_ in range(3):
            for h in product("ABCR", repeat=L_):
                d1 = dict(X0); d2 = g_apply(pi, tau, X0)
                for x_ in h:
                    y1 = fly.read(d1, None, x_); y2 = fly.read(d2, None, sig[x_])
                    if not C.pairing_ok(fly.comps(d1, y1, x_), fly.comps(d2, y2, sig[x_]), pmap):
                        ok_pair = False
                    _, d1, _, _ = C.step(fly, d1, None, x_, eb, "booked")
                    _, d2, _, _ = C.step(fly, d2, None, sig[x_], eb, "booked")
                    if g_apply(pi, tau, d1) != d2:
                        ok_eq = False
    check("T20c FLY model: every element of G4 satisfies process pairing (values preserved component by component) and, under booking as effective (ε = 1/10), is equivariant on all histories of length ≤ 2", ok_pair and ok_eq)
    Uu = M.fly_zero(); Uu[(0, "g1")] = F(1)
    Vv = M.fly_zero(); Vv[(0, "g1")] = Vv[(1, "g1")] = F(1, 2)
    sb, nb_ = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (Uu, None), (Vv, None), "ABR", 5, eb, "booked")
    sr, nr_ = C.exact_dyn_same(fly, (Uu, None), (Vv, None), "ABR", 5, F(0), "raw")
    check("T20d exact dynamical identity of U, V rerun under each semantics (%d ABR histories of length ≤ 5): original mechanism %s, booking as effective (ε = 1/10) %s" %
          (nb_, "holds" if sr else "does not hold", "holds" if sb else "does not hold"), sr and sb and nb_ == nr_)
    hce = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("p", F(1))]})
    _, dnum, _, _ = C.step(hce, {"x": F(1)}, None, "s", eb, "booked")
    cs_ce = hce.comps({"x": F(1)}, {}, "s")
    wF = F(1) + C.booked_sum_F(cs_ce["x"], eb, {"p": 0})
    check("T20e the two filtered-write operators: the same component +1 (> ε) with cert_eff = 0; the purely numerical filtered write W^ε_num gives post-state %s, the F-layer certified filtered write W^ε_F gives post-state %s" % (fs(dnum["x"]), fs(wF)),
          dnum["x"] == 2 and wF == 1)
    hpc = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: dict(d), lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("A", F(1, 5))], "z": [("A", F(1, 5))]})
    cpc = hpc.comps(d00, d00, "R")
    certA = {("A", "x"): 1, ("A", "z"): 0}
    numx, numz = C.booked_sum(cpc["x"], eb), C.booked_sum(cpc["z"], eb)
    Fx = C.booked_sum_F(cpc["x"], eb, {"A": certA[("A", "x")]}); Fz = C.booked_sum_F(cpc["z"], eb, {"A": certA[("A", "z")]})
    check("T20f a pairing that preserves values and thresholds but not certification: under the swap of x and z, W^ε_num commutes (%s, %s) and W^ε_F does not (%s, %s); cert_pairing_ok judges that it fails" %
          (fs(numx), fs(numz), fs(Fx), fs(Fz)),
          C.pairing_ok(cpc, cpc, sw) and numx == numz and Fx != Fz and not C.cert_pairing_ok(cpc, certA, certA, sw))
    # ---------------- T21 Post-processing and locality, non-invasiveness, hidden-change criterion (Theorems 18-3, 18-7) ----------------
    print("T21  Post-processing and locality, non-invasiveness, hidden-change criterion (Theorems 18-3, 18-7)")
    hp = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {}, lambda dn, do: {"x": dn["x"] + dn["z"], "z": dn["z"]})
    vx = [C.step(hp, {"x": F(0), "z": zz}, None, "R")[1]["x"] for zz in (F(0), F(1))]
    check("T21a cross-position post-processing post(d)_x = d_x + d_z: LW holds (identity write, constant local activity of x), yet changing only z changes Δd_x from %s to %s; the post-state conclusion of Theorem 18-3 needs the per-position post-processing antecedent" % (fs(vx[0]), fs(vx[1])),
          vx[0] != vx[1])
    hpw = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {}, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("w", F(1, 4))]},
                 lambda dn, do: {"x": min(dn["x"], F(1)), "z": dn["z"]})
    _, dpw, _, rpw = C.step(hpw, {"x": F(1), "z": F(0)}, None, "s")
    check("T21b non-invasiveness under saturating clipping: gate open, component 1/4 nonzero (Act⁰ = {x}), actual post-state d′ = d: non-invasive reading of the tendency is defined by the actual post-state",
          rpw["Act0"] == {"x"} and dpw == {"x": F(1), "z": F(0)})
    hsw = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"read_x": d["x"]}, lambda d, y, s: {}, lambda dn, do: {"x": dn["z"], "z": dn["x"]})
    arms = [{"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}, {"x": F(0), "z": F(1)}]
    ysw = [C.run(hsw, a_, None, ("R", "R"))[0] for a_ in arms]
    Bsw = [[F(0), F(1)], [F(1), F(0)]]
    naive = C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1), F(0)], [F(0), F(1)]], [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)])
    withB = C.hidden_forever_lti(Bsw, [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)])
    check("T21c affine post-processing (swapping the two positions), reading only x: the two states read the same in the first beat and separate in the second (%s vs %s); the criterion that omits post-processing (A_j = I) wrongly judges permanent hiding, and with its linear part B_j included it judges manifest" %
          (fs(ysw[0][1]["read_x"]), fs(ysw[1][1]["read_x"])),
          ysw[0][0] == ysw[1][0] and ysw[0][1] != ysw[1][1] and naive and not withB)
    hhalf = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"read_x": d["x"]}, lambda d, y, s: {}, lambda dn, do: {"x": dn["x"] / 2, "z": dn["z"] / 2})
    same_h, _ = C.exact_dyn_same(hhalf, (arms[0], None), (arms[1], None), "R", 8)
    check("T21d per-position affine post-processing (each position multiplied by 1/2), reading only x: the criterion with B_j = diag(1/2, 1/2) included judges permanent hiding, in agreement with an actual 8-beat run",
          same_h and C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1, 2), F(0)], [F(0), F(1, 2)]], [[F(1), F(0)]], [F(0), F(1)]))
    Ua2 = M.fly_zero(); Va2 = M.fly_zero()
    for i in M.ODOR["A"]:
        Ua2[(i, "g1")] = Va2[(i, "g1")] = F(3, 4)
    Ua2[(0, "a3")] = F(1); Va2[(0, "a3")] = Va2[(1, "a3")] = F(1, 2)
    yu = C.run(fly, Ua2, None, ("A", "A"))[0]; yv = C.run(fly, Va2, None, ("A", "A"))[0]
    check("T21e saturation (clipping) is not a common affine post-processing and does not fall under Theorem 18-7(1): Ua, Va read the same the first time and separate the second time (as in T2g)", yu[0] == yv[0] and yu[1] != yv[1])
    hsq = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"y": d["x"] + d["z"]},
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("square", d["x"] * d["x"]), ("rest", 1 - d["x"] * d["x"])]},
                 lambda dn, do: {k_: v_ / 8 for k_, v_ in dn.items()})
    st2 = ({"x": F(0), "z": F(1, 4)}, {"x": F(1, 4), "z": F(0)})
    pb = [C.step(hsq, d_, None, "R", eb, "booked") for d_ in st2]
    pr = [C.step(hsq, d_, None, "R", eb, "raw") for d_ in st2]
    nb2 = [hsq.read(q_[1], None, "R")["y"] for q_ in pb]
    nr2 = [hsq.read(q_[1], None, "R")["y"] for q_ in pr]
    crit = C.hidden_forever_lti([[F(1, 8), F(0)], [F(0), F(1, 8)]], [[F(1), F(1)]], [F(1, 4), F(-1, 4)])
    check("T21f the same selected set but a non-affine filtered write: components x², 1 − x² of x, ε = 1/10; on the domain [0, 1/4] both arms book only the latter; the original-mechanism slopes give A = I, B = I/8, and the criterion judges permanent hiding (%s); under the original mechanism the next-beat readings %s, %s are equal, under booking as effective %s, %s are separable" %
          (crit, fs(nr2[0]), fs(nr2[1]), fs(nb2[0]), fs(nb2[1])),
          pb[0][0] == pb[1][0] and pb[0][3]["Acte"] == pb[1][3]["Acte"] == {"x"} and crit and nr2[0] == nr2[1]
          and nb2 == [F(5, 32), F(19, 128)])
    # ---------------- T22 Contrast identity and certificate binding (Theorem 18-4, preamble) ----------------
    print("T22  Contrast identity and certificate binding: the declared contrast family computed contrast by contrast (Theorem 18-4, preamble)")
    lawJ = ("lin", F(1), F(3, 50), ("x", "z"))
    sJ = {"x": F(1), "z": F(1)}
    a1_, c1_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, {"x": [F(0)], "z": [F(0)]}, lambda _: 1)
    check("T22a single-set contrasts only (the single-set shorthand does not generate a joint contrast automatically): the two single-set differences are 3/50 each, numeric difference “%s”, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”" % (a1_["numeric difference"], a1_["complete certificate"], c1_),
          a1_["numeric difference"] == "numeric difference nonzero, below threshold" and a1_["complete certificate"] == 0 and c1_ == "functional support established")
    famJ = {"contrast": [{"id": "x single-set", "set": {"x": F(0)}}, {"id": "z single-set", "set": {"z": F(0)}},
                     {"id": "x, z joint", "set": {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    a2_, c2_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famJ, lambda _: 1)
    check("T22b valid joint contrast (the joint contrast declared explicitly as one entry): joint difference 3/25 > ε, witness contrast “%s”, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”" % (a2_["witness contrast"], a2_["complete certificate"], c2_),
          a2_["complete certificate"] == 1 and a2_["witness contrast"] == "x, z joint" and c2_ == "effective difference certified")
    famM = {"contrast": [{"id": "x single-set", "set": {"x": F(0)}, "evidence": {"route": 1}},
                     {"id": "x, z joint", "set": {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}, "evidence": {"route": "⊥"}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    a3_, c3_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famM, lambda _: 0)
    check("T22c difference and certificate mismatched: the threshold-crossing difference belongs to the joint contrast, whose route is unproven, while the route certificate belongs to the single-set contrast, whose difference 3/50 does not cross the threshold; per-contrast results %s, not spliced together; complete certificate %s" %
          ([(k, fs(v), cc) for k, v, cc, _ in a3_["contrast details"]], a3_["complete certificate"]),
          a3_["complete certificate"] == "⊥" and a3_["witness contrast"] is None and c3_ != "effective difference certified")
    famS = {"contrast": [{"id": "x, z joint", "set": {"x": F(0), "z": F(0)}, "evidence": {"route": 1}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    a4_, c4_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famS, lambda _: 0)
    check("T22d complete within one contrast: the threshold-crossing difference and the route certificate belong to the same joint contrast; complete certificate %s, witness contrast “%s”" % (a4_["complete certificate"], a4_["witness contrast"]),
          a4_["complete certificate"] == 1 and a4_["witness contrast"] == "x, z joint")
    famI = {"contrast": [{"id": "x single-set", "set": {"x": F(0)}}], "coverage": "incomplete"}
    a5_, c5_ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famI, lambda _: 0)
    check("T22e “numerically below threshold” is kept apart from “contrast domain insufficiently covered”: when the declared coverage is incomplete and no listed contrast crosses the threshold, “%s” is recorded, summary “%s”, not read as a negation" % (a5_["numeric difference"], c5_),
          a5_["numeric difference"] == "contrast domain insufficiently covered" and c5_ == "effective difference not certified")
    lawP = ("lin", F(1), F(1), ("x", "z"))
    sP = {"x": F(1), "z": F(1)}
    famZ = {"contrast": [{"id": "z single-set", "set": {"z": F(0)}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    a6_, c6_ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famZ, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    check("T22f aggregate path true, path from the actually changed origin false: c = 1 + x + z, only x has a certified path, and the frozen contrast sets only z to zero; aggregate Path = %s (an independent atom), z single-set difference %s, route of that contrast %s, complete certificate %s, summary “%s”" %
          (a6_["Path"], fs(a6_["contrast details"][0][1]), a6_["contrast details"][0][3]["route"], a6_["complete certificate"], c6_),
          a6_["Path"] == 1 and a6_["contrast details"][0][3]["route"] == "⊥" and a6_["complete certificate"] == "⊥" and c6_ == "functional support established")
    famZ2 = {"contrast": [{"id": "z single-set", "set": {"z": F(0)}, "evidence": {"route": 1}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    a7_, c7_ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famZ2, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    check("T22g the same contrast has a separately proven actual route: the z single-set contrast carries its own route certificate 1; complete certificate %s, witness contrast “%s”, its four clauses %s" %
          (a7_["complete certificate"], a7_["witness contrast"], a7_["Def. 6′ four clauses"]),
          a7_["complete certificate"] == 1 and a7_["witness contrast"] == "z single-set" and a7_["Def. 6′ four clauses"]["route"] == 1)
    famI2 = {"contrast": [{"id": "x single-set", "set": {"x": F(0)}}], "coverage": "incomplete"}
    a8_, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawJ, sJ, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famI2, lambda _: 1)
    check("T22h listed separately when coverage is incomplete: certificate status “%s”" % a8_["certificate status"], a8_["certificate status"] == "disjunction of listed contrast certificates = 0; declared domain not yet covered")
    famE1 = {"contrast": [{"id": "z single-set", "set": {"z": F(0)}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    famE2 = {"contrast": [{"id": "z single-set (x also written at its original value)", "set": {"x": F(1), "z": F(0)}}], "coverage": "complete"}
    e1, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famE1, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    e2, _ = C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"}, famE2, lambda st: 1 if "x" in st else 0)
    check("T22i two encodings of the same contrast state: {z: 0} and {x: 1, z: 0} (x was already 1, unchanged) give the same certificate after normalization to the actual difference set (%s, %s); the unchanged x does not enter the origins" %
          (e1["complete certificate"], e2["complete certificate"]), e1["complete certificate"] == e2["complete certificate"] == "⊥"
          and e1["contrast details"][0][3] == e2["contrast details"][0][3])
    try:
        C.readback_atoms(lawP, sP, {"x", "z"}, eb, {"x", "z"},
                         {"contrast": [{"id": "k", "set": {"z": F(0)}}, {"id": "k", "set": {"x": F(0)}}]}, lambda st: 1)
        dup = False
    except ValueError:
        dup = True
    check("T22j contrast identities must be unique: two contrasts with the same name are rejected; the four clauses of the certified entry are stored directly, not via a name-keyed dictionary", dup)
    # ---------------- T23 J₁₈: the three conditions via the [S2] evaluator (Theorem 18-4(4)) ----------------
    print("T23  The three conditions of J₁₈ via the [S2] evaluator field_final: check D20, reproduce the trajectory, evaluate D19 qualification (Theorem 18-4(4))")
    w = (1, 2)
    def j18c(*a, **k):
        """Cases T23a–j: the evidence protocol declares the evidence graph closed."""
        return C.j18_check(*a, closed_graph=True, **k)
    pulse_x = {"kind": "pulse", "name": "S0", "beat": 0, "target": "x", "amount": F(1, 2), "out-of-boundary": True, "occurs": 1}
    def proc(name, t, c_eff=1, c_read=1, k=F(1, 4), b=F(0), law="linear", reads=("x",), inside=True):
        return {"kind": "process", "name": name, "beat": t, "target": "x", "carrier inside": inside, "law": law, "reads": reads,
                "coefficient": k, "bias": b, "cert_eff": c_eff, "cert_read": c_read}
    lk = [("state:x:1", "P1", 1)]
    jz = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, c_eff="⊥", k=F(0), b=F(0), law="constant", reads=())])
    check("T23a zero component but action certification undetermined: D20 %s; F is not run, %s" % (jz["D20"], jz["R"]),
          jz["D20"].startswith("reject") and jz["event table"] == 0 and jz["Q_F"] == "not run")
    ju = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, c_eff="⊥")], lk)
    check("T23b nonzero component but action certification undetermined: D20 %s; R layer pending, reported separately" % ju["D20"], ju["D20"].startswith("reject") and ju["R"].startswith("R layer pending"))
    j0 = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, c_eff=0, k=F(0), b=F(1), law="constant", reads=())])
    check("T23c cert_eff = 0 (decided, D20 accepts): constant component +1, original-mechanism trajectory %s, F trajectory %s, trajectory condition %s; F atom Act_F = %s" %
          ([fs(a["x"]) for a in j0["original-mechanism trajectory"]], [fs(F(a["x"])) for a in j0["F trajectory"]], j0["trajectory"], j0["events"][0]["Act_F"]),
          j0["D20"] == "accept" and j0["trajectory"] == 0 and j0["events"][0]["Act_F"] == 0)
    jt = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",),
                     [{"kind": "process", "name": "G0", "beat": 0, "target": "x", "carrier inside": True, "law": "constant", "reads": (), "bias": F(1, 2)},
                      proc("P1", 1)], [("state:x:1", "P1", 1)])
    check("T23d a qualified action but Trace_F = 0: x is formed by in-field continuation with no out-of-boundary cause (Mark empty); P1 has Act_F = %s, HistCond_F = %s; Trace_F = %s, Q_F = %s" %
          (jt["events"][0]["Act_F"], jt["events"][0]["HistCond_F"], jt["Trace_F"], jt["Q_F"]),
          jt["events"][0]["Act_F"] == 1 and jt["events"][0]["HistCond_F"] == 1 and jt["Trace_F"] == 0 and jt["Q_F"] == 0)
    jr = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("R1", 1, k=F(0), b=F(0))])
    check("T23e pure read: component 0, read certified, Cond_F = %s, Act_F = %s, Part_F = %s; trajectory %s" %
          (jr["events"][0]["Cond_F"], jr["events"][0]["Act_F"], jr["Part_F"], jr["trajectory"]),
          jr["events"][0]["Cond_F"] == 1 and jr["events"][0]["Act_F"] == 0 and jr["Part_F"] == 1 and jr["trajectory"] == 1)
    js = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",),
                     [dict(pulse_x, beat=1), proc("P1", 1)], [("state:x:1", "P1", 1)])
    jp = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1)], lk)
    check("T23f Form must precede the current beat and have a source: with the out-of-boundary pulse and the read both at beat 1, HistCond_F = %s; with the out-of-boundary pulse at beat 0 (before the read, source out-of-boundary), HistCond_F = %s, Trace_F = %s" %
          (js["events"][0]["HistCond_F"], jp["events"][0]["HistCond_F"], jp["Trace_F"]),
          js["events"][0]["HistCond_F"] == 0 and jp["events"][0]["HistCond_F"] == 1 and jp["Trace_F"] == 1)
    check("T23g positive case on the same table: D20 accepts, the original-mechanism trajectory %s agrees with the F trajectory beat by beat, Trace_F = Part_F = HDyn_F = Q_F = 1" %
          [fs(a["x"]) for a in jp["original-mechanism trajectory"]],
          jp["D20"] == "accept" and jp["trajectory"] == 1 and jp["Trace_F"] == jp["Part_F"] == jp["HDyn_F"] == jp["Q_F"] == 1)
    rb_ = C.readback_atoms(("lin", F(0), F(1, 4), ("x",)), {"x": F(1, 2)}, {"x"}, eb, {"x"}, {"x": [F(0)]}, lambda _: 1)[0]
    check("T23h cross-check of the atoms of one event on both sides: this paper's read-back (pre-state x = 1/2 at beat 1) gives Act_F = %s, HistCond_F = %s, the same as Act_F = %s, HistCond_F = %s of event P1 in the F evaluator" %
          (rb_["Act_F"], rb_["HistCond_F"], jp["events"][0]["Act_F"], jp["events"][0]["HistCond_F"]),
          (rb_["Act_F"], rb_["HistCond_F"]) == (jp["events"][0]["Act_F"], jp["events"][0]["HistCond_F"]) == (1, 1))
    jn = j18c(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",), [pulse_x, proc("P1", 1, k=F(-3), b=F(0))], lk)
    check("T23i a negative update falls outside D20: the component −3/2 makes the updated value negative, D20 %s" % jn["D20"], jn["D20"].startswith("reject"))
    enc, back = C.nonneg_encode({"x": F(-1, 2), "z": F(1, 4)})
    check("T23j nonnegative encoding of signed depth: d = d⁺ − d⁻, all components nonnegative, read-back restores (−1/2, 1/4)",
          all(v >= 0 for v in enc.values()) and back(enc) == {"x": F(-1, 2), "z": F(1, 4)})
    jdec = C.j18_check(("x",), (0.2,), (0,), F(1, 100), ("x",), [{"kind": "pulse", "name": "u", "beat": 0, "target": "x", "amount": 0.1}], closed_graph=True)
    check("T23k decimal pulse: initial value 0.2 and a same-beat pulse 0.1, both arms interpret the decimals exactly; F trajectory %s, original-mechanism trajectory %s, trajectory condition %s" %
          (fs(F(jdec["F trajectory"][0]["x"])), fs(jdec["original-mechanism trajectory"][0]["x"]), jdec["trajectory"]),
          jdec["D20"] == "accept" and jdec["trajectory"] == 1 and jdec["original-mechanism trajectory"][0]["x"] == F(3, 10))
    jdc = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), w, eb, ("x",),
                      [dict(pulse_x, amount=0.5), dict(proc("P1", 1), coefficient=0.1, bias=0.3)], lk, closed_graph=True)
    check("T23l decimal read/write coefficients: pulse 0.5, linear-law coefficient 0.1, bias 0.3; the original-mechanism trajectory %s agrees with the F trajectory beat by beat" %
          [fs(a["x"]) for a in jdc["original-mechanism trajectory"]], jdc["D20"] == "accept" and jdc["trajectory"] == 1)
    check("T23m the existing genuine inequality case is unchanged: with cert_eff = 0 the trajectory condition is 0 (T23c)", j0["trajectory"] == 0)
    pulse_m = dict(pulse_x, amount=F(1, 2), beat=-1)
    proc_m = {"kind": "process", "name": "p", "beat": 0, "target": "x", "carrier inside": True, "law": "linear", "reads": ("x",),
              "coefficient": F(1), "bias": F(0)}
    jo = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m], closed_graph=False)
    jc = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m], closed_graph=True)
    jl = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m], [("state:x:0", "p", 1)], closed_graph=False)
    jun = C.j18_check(("x",), (F(0),), (0,), eb, ("x",), [pulse_m, proc_m])
    check("T23n the closed-graph condition is declared by the evidence protocol: open graph without a path HDyn_F = %s, closed graph %s, with a certified path %s; undeclared: %s" %
          (jo["HDyn_F"], jc["HDyn_F"], jl["HDyn_F"], jun["D20"]),
          jo["HDyn_F"] == "⊥" and jc["HDyn_F"] == 0 and jl["HDyn_F"] == 1 and jun["D20"].startswith("reject"))
    # ---------------- T24 The law layer actually taking effect, and difference transfer (Theorem 18-8) ----------------
    print("T24  The law layer actually taking effect, and difference transfer (Theorem 18-8(2)–(5))")
    _, h1b, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H1, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    _, h2b, _, _ = C.step(flyc, H2, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    check("T24a gating not booked: under booking as effective with ε_L = 1 the 0→1 consolidation component is not booked, the c difference persists after the strong-write beat (c(0) = %s vs %s), and no overwriting occurs; under the original mechanism the same beat overwrites (T15d)" %
          (fs(h1b[("c", 0)]), fs(h2b[("c", 0)])), h1b[("c", 0)] != h2b[("c", 0)] and h1n == h2n)
    htr = C.Host(["x", "z"], lambda d, r, s: {"read_z": d["z"]},
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("clear", -d["x"])], "z": [("transfer", d["x"])]})
    t1 = C.run(htr, {"x": F(1, 2), "z": F(0)}, None, ("s", "s"))
    t2 = C.run(htr, {"x": F(1, 4), "z": F(0)}, None, ("s", "s"))
    check("T24b difference transfer: the arms have x depths 1/2 and 1/4; after one beat x is 0 in both and the difference has moved to z; reading z in the second beat gives %s vs %s: a readable difference does not require the difference to persist at its original position" %
          (fs(t1[0][1]["read_z"]), fs(t2[0][1]["read_z"])),
          t1[1]["x"] == t2[1]["x"] == 0 and t1[0][1] != t2[0][1])
    _, wk1, _, _ = C.step(flyc, G1, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    _, wk2, _, _ = C.step(flyc, G2, None, "A", F(1), "booked")
    check("T24c under booking as effective with ε_L = 1 a weak write is not booked: after one beat both arms have α3 depth %s, so the actual-post-state-difference antecedent of (5) fails" % fs(wk1[(0, "a3")]),
          wk1[(0, "a3")] == wk2[(0, "a3")] == 0)
    hl = C.Host(["x", "c"], lambda d, r, s: {"r": F(1)},
                lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - (F(1, 2) if d["c"] == 0 else F(9, 10))) * d["x"])], "c": [("set", 1 - d["c"])]})
    pl = [C.step(hl, {"x": F(0), "c": F(cc)}, None, "s") for cc in (0, 1)]
    check("T24d the first whole-event difference can fall on c first: the arms have x = 0 and c = 0 and 1, and a strong gate sets c in the same beat; relaxation components both %s, setting components %s vs %s" %
          (fs(pl[0][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1]), fs(pl[0][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1]), fs(pl[1][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1])),
          pl[0][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == pl[1][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == 0 and pl[0][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1] != pl[1][3]["comps"]["c"][0][1])
    def halfpost(dn, do):
        return {k_: v_ / 2 for k_, v_ in dn.items()}
    arms_h = []
    for rho_ in (F(1, 2), F(9, 10)):
        hx = C.Host(["x"], lambda d, r, s: {}, (lambda rr: (lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - rr) * d["x"])]}))(rho_), halfpost)
        arms_h.append(C.step(hx, {"x": F(1, 2)}, None, "s")[1]["x"])
    comp_diff = (F(9, 10) - F(1, 2)) * F(1, 2)
    check("T24e per-position post(v)_x = v_x/2 (no truncation, no gridding): component difference %s, actual post-state difference %s (halved); the actual post-state difference must be computed from both arms' values after their actual post-processing" %
          (fs(comp_diff), fs(arms_h[1] - arms_h[0])), arms_h[1] - arms_h[0] == comp_diff / 2)
    hr0 = C.Host(["x", "c"], lambda d, r, s: r, lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - (F(1, 2) if d["c"] == 0 else F(9, 10))) * d["x"])]})
    pr0 = [C.step(hr0, {"x": F(0), "c": F(cc)}, F(cc), "R") for cc in (0, 1)]
    check("T24f excluded case: same d, different r (d_x = 0, c = 0 and 1, initial r = 0 and 1, Φ = r): the first-beat readings are already %s vs %s while the relaxation components are both 0 — Theorem 18-8 requires the two arms to have the same initial complete state (including r) apart from c" %
          (fs(pr0[0][0]), fs(pr0[1][0])), pr0[0][0] != pr0[1][0] and pr0[0][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == pr0[1][3]["comps"]["x"][0][1] == 0)
    hr1 = C.Host(["x", "c"], lambda d, r, s: {"y": d["x"] + r},
                 lambda d, y, s: {"x": [("relax", -(1 - (F(1, 2) if d["c"] == 0 else F(9, 10))) * d["x"])]},
                 None, lambda r, y: r / 2 + y["y"] / 4)
    ra = C.run(hr1, {"x": F(1, 2), "c": F(0)}, F(1, 2), ("R", "R"))[0]
    rb2 = C.run(hr1, {"x": F(1, 2), "c": F(1)}, F(1, 2), ("R", "R"))[0]
    check("T24g positive case: equal initial residue (r = 1/2) and the same residue recursion P(r, y) = r/2 + y/4 in both arms, which does not read c; the first-beat readings are both %s; after the first relaxation-component difference at the target depth, the second-beat readings are %s vs %s" %
          (fs(ra[0]["y"]), fs(ra[1]["y"]), fs(rb2[1]["y"])), ra[0] == rb2[0] and ra[1] != rb2[1])
    # ---------------- T25 Supplementary proof witnesses (Theorems 18-1(2), 18-2(3), 18-7(5)) ----------------
    print("T25  Supplementary proof witnesses (Theorems 18-1(2), 18-2(3), 18-7(5))")
    check("T25a different O but the same Φ: two states with the same d and different c (Φ does not read c) have the same full activity under every signal (the two states of T8a), yet their labeled organization descriptions differ",
          P0 != P1 and all(M.flyc_read(P0, None, x_) == M.flyc_read(P1, None, x_) for x_ in "ABCR"))
    check("T25b different Φ but the same y under a fixed actual signal: the two tube-network states have the same full activity under the actual injection and differ under the node-2 probe (the two states of T1d)",
          QXa == QYa and pXa == pYa and pXb[2] != pYb[2])
    ms1 = sorted(M.fly_read(Xs, None, "A").values())
    ms_all = {x_: sorted(M.fly_read(Xt, None, x_).values()) for x_ in "ABC"}
    check("T25c under the γ1↔α3 swap the read-out commutation formula fails for every signal permutation σ_g and activity-coordinate permutation ψ: the value multiset %s of Φ(d, A) differs from each value multiset of Φ(π·d, s′) (s′ = A, B, C)" %
          [fs(v) for v in ms1], all(ms1 != m_ for m_ in ms_all.values()))
    rN, rP = F(4, 5), F(0)
    yy = [2 * (F(1, 2) * rN ** n_ - F(1, 2) * rP ** n_) for n_ in range(1, 6)]
    check("T25d parallel opposition with one branch at shallowing rate 0: the difference y(n) = 2d(r_N^n − r_P^n) is never 0 for n ≥ 1 (no ratio used)", all(v != 0 for v in yy))
    print("=" * 60)
    nb = RES.count(False)
    print("Total %d items: %d pass, %d mismatch" % (len(RES), len(RES) - nb, nb))
    return 1 if nb else 0

def ETA_A():
    return M.ETA * M.A_INN

def ETA_B():
    return M.ETA * M.B_DIS

if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
~~~

A.4  field\_final.py  The F-layer evaluator (D10–D20) of Appendix A.1 of \[S2\], *The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization* v1.0, verbatim identical to that appendix

~~~python
"""The Mathematical Form of Field Concretization v1.0, Appendix A: F-layer (finite separable layer) evaluator.
Formal simulation, not certification of material causation.
"""
from dataclasses import dataclass
from math import isfinite
from collections import defaultdict
from fractions import Fraction
Tri = bool | None
def exact(v):
    """Each input number is taken as the exact rational given by its decimal representation; all F-layer arithmetic is carried out exactly over the rationals."""
    return Fraction(repr(v)) if isinstance(v, float) else Fraction(v)
def tri_and(*xs):
    if False in xs:return False
    if None in xs:return None
    return True
def tri_or(xs):
    xs=list(xs)
    if True in xs:return True
    if None in xs:return None
    return False
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Pulse:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; amount:float; external:bool=True; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Process:
    name:str; step:int; target:str; carrier_inside:bool; law:str
    reads:tuple[str,...]=(); coefficient:float=0.; bias:float=0.
    certified_effect:Tri=True; certified_read:Tri=True
    # rule: constant, linear (sum of read sites), bilinear (two sites)
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Link:
    source:str; target:str; certified:Tri=True
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Spec:
    sites:tuple[str,...]; initial:tuple[float,...]; window:tuple[int,...]
    pulses:tuple[Pulse,...]; processes:tuple[Process,...]
    links:tuple[Link,...]=(); identity:Tri=True; trace_history:Tri=True
    closed_graph:bool=True; epsilon:float=1e-9
    declared_T:tuple[str,...]|None=None

def _num(v, nonneg=False):
    # Legal numbers: integers, rationals (Fraction), or finite decimal floats
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, (int, float, Fraction)): return False
    if isinstance(v, float) and not isfinite(v): return False
    return (not nonneg) or v >= 0
def check(x):
    if not x.sites or len(set(x.sites))!=len(x.sites):raise ValueError('sites')
    if x.declared_T is None or not x.declared_T or len(set(x.declared_T))!=len(x.declared_T) or any(s not in x.sites for s in x.declared_T):raise ValueError('declared T')
    if not x.window or tuple(range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+1))!=x.window:raise ValueError('window')
    if len(x.initial)!=len(x.sites) or not all(_num(z,True) for z in x.initial):raise ValueError('initial')
    if not _num(x.epsilon,True):raise ValueError('epsilon')
    if x.identity not in (True,False,None) or x.trace_history not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('identity')
    names=set()
    for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes):
        if not e.name or e.name in names:raise ValueError('event id')
        names.add(e.name)
        if e.step<x.window[0]-1 or e.step>x.window[-1]:raise ValueError('event time')
        if e.target not in x.sites:raise ValueError('target')
        if isinstance(e,Pulse):
            if not _num(e.amount,True):raise ValueError('pulse amount')
            # In F, certified is the certification of “whether the pulse actually occurs”: 1 = occurs, 0 = does not occur; ⊥ is outside the single-trajectory domain of F and must be enumerated by branch in R
            if e.certified is None:raise ValueError('F_UNDECIDED_PULSE: enumerate occurrence branches in R')
            if e.certified not in (True,False):raise ValueError('pulse evidence')
        else:
            if e.law not in ('constant','linear','bilinear'):raise ValueError('law')
            if any(s not in x.sites for s in e.reads):raise ValueError('read sites')
            if e.law=='bilinear' and len(e.reads)!=2:raise ValueError('bilinear arity')
            if e.law=='linear' and not e.reads:raise ValueError('linear reads')
            if e.law=='constant' and e.reads:raise ValueError('constant reads')
            if not _num(e.coefficient) or not _num(e.bias):raise ValueError('params')
            # D20: the action certification must be decided; a mechanism with undetermined action is outside the F domain (even when its component is zero)
            if e.certified_effect not in (True,False):raise ValueError('undecided mechanism effect')
            if e.certified_read not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('evidence')
    # All trace nodes are snapshots at integer t; process nodes at integer t+0.5.
    # Strict temporal order prevents fabricated same-step read-after-write.
    times={f'state:{s}:{t}':float(t) for s in x.sites for t in range(x.window[0],x.window[-1]+2)}
    times.update({e.name:float(e.step)+0.5 for e in (*x.pulses,*x.processes)})
    for link in x.links:
        if link.source not in times or link.target not in times:raise ValueError('link endpoint')
        if times[link.source]>=times[link.target]:raise ValueError('noncausal link')
        if link.certified not in (True,False,None):raise ValueError('link evidence')
    return times
def path_status(links,starts,target,closed=True):
    adjacency=defaultdict(list)
    for l in links:adjacency[l.source].append(l)
    def reachable(allow):
        seen=set(starts);queue=list(starts)
        while queue:
            u=queue.pop()
            for l in adjacency[u]:
                if allow(l.certified) and l.target not in seen:
                    seen.add(l.target);queue.append(l.target)
        return target in seen
    if reachable(lambda w:w is True):return True
    if reachable(lambda w:w is not False):return None
    return False if closed else None
def run(x):
    check(x)
    eps=exact(x.epsilon)
    state={s:exact(v) for s,v in zip(x.sites,x.initial)}
    # For each site, certified external history must be identified as event, not inferred from nonzero state.
    formation=defaultdict(list); history_snapshots=defaultdict(list)
    signal_marks=defaultdict(list)  # out-of-boundary cause: changes left at this position by certified, effective out-of-boundary action components (pulses, or processes whose carrier is outside the boundary)
    part=[];hdyn=[];reports=[]
    for t in range(x.window[0]-1,x.window[-1]+1):
        before=dict(state); contributions=defaultdict(list);events=[]
        # Pulse first as an event, but processes read the prior snapshot, not pulse-modified value.
        for p in x.pulses:
            if p.step!=t:continue
            # Count only pulses certified as occurring and effective; a pulse confirmed not to occur contributes nothing and registers no formation or cause
            a=exact(p.amount)
            if p.certified is True and a>eps:
                contributions[p.target].append((p.name,a))
                formation[p.target].append((p.name,t,p.external))
                if p.external:signal_marks[p.target].append((p.name,t))
        for m in x.processes:
            if m.step!=t:continue
            k=exact(m.coefficient); b=exact(m.bias)
            if m.law=='constant':value=b
            elif m.law=='linear':value=b+k*sum(before[s] for s in m.reads)
            else:value=b+k*before[m.reads[0]]*before[m.reads[1]]
            is_action=tri_and(abs(value)>eps,m.certified_effect)
            # Declared reads do not establish a genuine dependence: compute the law's support.
            support=()
            # Support for the organizational condition: the numerical law depends on the read positions, or the process is a pure read (component not an effective change, |c| ≤ ε); read certification enters as a separate conjunct, and an undetermined value is not truncated to false in advance
            # Functional support is decided by a mathematically nonzero partial derivative (coefficient and partner reading exactly nonzero), not conflated with the effective-change threshold ε
            if m.law=='linear' and (k!=0 or abs(value)<=eps): support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            # For every element in the declared T, historical formation must precede the read.
            active_T=set(x.declared_T)
            available=[s for s in support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            is_cond=tri_and(bool(available),m.certified_read)
            # No automatic path from a read-site to an act. Explicit event links are required.
            old_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in available]
            route=path_status(x.links,old_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if old_nodes else False
            # Action provenance must be supported by a nonzero functional dependence,
            # not merely a certified read that has no effect on the action.
            action_support=()
            if m.law=='linear' and k!=0:action_support=m.reads
            elif m.law=='bilinear' and k!=0:
                action_support=tuple(s for i,s in enumerate(m.reads) if before[m.reads[1-i]]!=0)
            action_available=[s for s in action_support if s in active_T and before[s]>eps and any(a[1]<t for a in formation[s])]
            action_nodes=[f'state:{s}:{t}' for s in action_available]
            action_route=path_status(x.links,action_nodes,m.name,x.closed_graph) if action_nodes else False
            is_hist=tri_and(bool(action_available),m.certified_read,action_route)
            if is_action is True:
                contributions[m.target].append((m.name,value))
                # Formation of a new element: with its carrier inside B it is formation by in-field continuation; with its carrier outside the boundary it is formation by signal
                if value>eps and before[m.target]<=eps:
                    formation[m.target].append((m.name,t,not m.carrier_inside))
                # An effective component of a process whose carrier is outside the boundary is a change left by a signal, whether the element is new or old and whether the component is positive or negative
                if not m.carrier_inside:
                    signal_marks[m.target].append((m.name,t))
            elif is_action is None: # defensive check: an undetermined action certification is already excluded from the domain by check()
                raise ValueError('unresolved effect cannot determine numeric trajectory')
            if t in x.window:
                if m.carrier_inside:part.append(is_cond)
                hdyn.append(tri_and(m.target in active_T,is_action,is_hist))
            events.append({'id':m.name,'delta':value,'act':is_action,'cond':is_cond,'histcond':is_hist,'route':route})
        for s in x.sites:
            new=before[s]+sum(v for _,v in contributions[s])
            # Depth must not be negative: an exact updated value below zero leaves the domain; no truncation to zero, no tolerance
            if new < 0:raise ValueError('negative trace depth')
            state[s]=new
            if state[s]>eps and formation[s]:history_snapshots[s].append(t+1)
        if t in x.window:reports.append({'step':t,'before':before,'after':dict(state),'contributions':dict(contributions),'events':events})
    ts=x.declared_T
    eligible=all(state[s]>eps and formation[s] for s in ts)
    sources={origin for site in ts for origin,t in signal_marks[site]}
    trace=tri_and(bool(sources),eligible,x.identity,x.trace_history)
    pw=tri_or(part); hw=tri_or(hdyn)
    return {'trace':trace,'part':pw,'hdyn':hw,'Q':tri_and(trace,pw,hw),'reports':reports,'physical_certificate':False}
~~~

## Appendix B  Run Results

B.1  Runtime Environment and Checksums  
Runtime environment: Linux 6.18 x86\_64; Python 3.13, standard library only. The command of B.2 is run with python3 -E -s -B in an empty directory containing only the files of Appendix A; it exits with code 0 and an empty standard error, and the output is deterministic (two runs are verbatim identical; it is listed verbatim in B.2). Local run time is about 3 seconds (wall clock, varying with the machine).
